MODELICA RESEARCH LIBRARY · LEARNING GUIDE 1.0

EvaluationLibrary

从仿真曲线到带资格的指标、判定与输出文件From simulation traces to qualified metrics, verdicts and output files

v0.3.170 个功能定义70 functional definitions10 个案例10 examples38 个辅助定义38 supporting definitions

固定源码：Pinned source: 18fb8c3ca0bf

01

学习目标与阅读路线Learning objectives and reading route

EvaluationLibrary 为已经存在的仿真信号增加只读观测层。它计算数值指标、检查明确需求、比较配对结果，并把结果组织为表格、综合判定、CSV、文本或命令台输出。数值与支持该数值的证据状态同时保留。EvaluationLibrary adds a read-only observation layer to existing simulation signals. It calculates metrics, checks explicit requirements, compares paired results and organises tables, aggregate verdicts, CSV, text or terminal output. Numerical values are retained alongside their evidence qualification.

学习本库不能只学“哪个模块输出多少”。必须先定义观察什么、在哪个窗口观察、哪些输入有效、采样如何安排、什么条件算违规，然后才能解释结果。v0.3.1 新增恢复的结果输出链和完成日志也在本指南中单独讲解。Learning this library requires more than knowing which block outputs a number. Define the quantity, observation window, validity, sampling and violation criterion before interpreting results. The reporting chain restored in v0.3.1 and its completion logs are covered explicitly.

本指南依据固定提交的源码编写。示例中的数值与曲线描述为源码推导的复现参考，未在本次文档工作中重新运行 Modelica 仿真。This guide documents a fixed source revision. Numerical and curve descriptions are source-derived reproduction references; Modelica simulations were not re-run during this documentation work.
阶段Stage操作与学习目标Action and learning objective
01先用 AnalyticStatistics 手算参考值，区分时间加权统计与显式采样统计。Start with AnalyticStatistics and analytical reference values, distinguishing time-weighted from sampled statistics.
02学习 BoundsAndValidWitness 与 IncompleteWindow，正确理解有效性、完整性与违规证据。Study BoundsAndValidWitness and IncompleteWindow to interpret validity, completeness and violation witnesses.
03按任务选择响应时间、配对比较或频域指标，并记录窗口和采样网格。Choose response timing, paired comparison or frequency measures, recording windows and sample grids.
04最后接入 Results 表格与写出器，核对元数据、实际路径和 SUCCESS/SKIPPED 日志。Connect Results tables and writers last, checking metadata, actual paths and SUCCESS/SKIPPED logs.

建议先掌握一个完整示例，再按问题查找元件。每张元件卡列出实际参数和接口，并提供去除图形注解的源码；辅助类与可直接实例化的元件分别列出。Begin with one complete example, then select components by the question being studied. Each component card contains actual parameters, interfaces and source with graphical annotations omitted. Supporting classes are distinguished from directly instantiable components.

02

理解元件之前，先理解这些概念Concepts to understand before using the components

有数值、已结束、完整和满足是四件事A value, finalisation, completeness and satisfaction are different

valueAvailable 说明数值可解释；complete 说明观测已经结束；dataStatus=COMPLETE 说明覆盖符合规则；verdict=SATISFIED 还需要明确需求判据。不能将 complete=true 或数值不为零当成需求满足。valueAvailable qualifies a numerical value. complete indicates finalisation. dataStatus=COMPLETE indicates adequate coverage. verdict=SATISFIED additionally requires an explicit requirement criterion. Neither complete=true nor a non-zero value establishes satisfaction.

状态与判定有独立枚举Data status and verdict have separate enumerations

DataStatus 包含 PENDING、COMPLETE、INCOMPLETE、INVALID、NOT_APPLICABLE；Verdict 包含 UNDECIDED、SATISFIED、VIOLATED、INVALID、NOT_APPLICABLE。无效数据优先影响判定，但已观察到的 everViolated 与 firstViolationTime 可继续保留。DataStatus contains PENDING, COMPLETE, INCOMPLETE, INVALID and NOT_APPLICABLE. Verdict contains UNDECIDED, SATISFIED, VIOLATED, INVALID and NOT_APPLICABLE. Invalid data take precedence in the verdict while an earlier everViolated witness and firstViolationTime can remain recorded.

窗口与内部采样定义证据范围The window and internal sampling define evidence scope

固定窗口按 [windowStart,windowStop) 解释。ExternalSingle 只接受第一个外部窗口，再次开启会标为无效。samplePeriod 和 sampleStart 定义内部采样；求解器输出 Interval 只是结果保存网格，两者不能混同。Fixed windows use [windowStart,windowStop). ExternalSingle accepts the first external window and flags a later reopening as invalid. samplePeriod and sampleStart define internal sampling; solver output Interval is a result-recording grid, not the same mechanism.

终点前未响应不一定已经超时An unanswered request at observation end is not necessarily a timeout

请求尚未达到 deadline 就遇到观测终点，属于删失：不知道它之后能否及时响应。RepeatedResponse 使用有限队列区分匹配、超时、未匹配响应与删失；这些计数不能合并成一种失败。A request whose deadline lies after observation end is censored: timely future response is unknown. RepeatedResponse uses a finite queue to distinguish matches, timeouts, unmatched responses and censoring. These are not interchangeable failure counts.

元数据不会随连接器自动全部传递Not all metadata travel through the connector

MetricInput/MetricOutput 传递数值、状态、判定和若干覆盖字段；metricId、unitName、requirementVersion 与窗口元数据由 ResultTable 或写出器参数提供。两处要显式保持一致。v0.3.1 的 records.observedDuration 设为 0 占位，因为连接器没有该字段；它不是测得的观测时长。MetricInput/MetricOutput carry values, status, verdict and selected coverage fields. metricId, unitName, requirementVersion and window metadata are parameters of ResultTable or writers and must be kept consistent explicitly. In v0.3.1, records.observedDuration is a zero placeholder because the connector lacks that field; it is not a measured duration.

输出完成日志不是仿真验证证据A completion log is not simulation validation evidence

写出器默认在结果完成后执行一次；文件关闭并确认存在后才打印 SUCCESS，已有文件按 RejectExisting 跳过则打印 SKIPPED。generated_at 是运行主机当地时间，simulation_time 是仿真时刻。成功写文件只说明输出操作完成，不说明所有需求都通过。Writers perform a one-shot action after result finalisation. SUCCESS follows file closure and existence confirmation; RejectExisting produces SKIPPED for an existing file. generated_at is host-local wall time and simulation_time is model time. Successful writing does not mean that all requirements passed.

因果信号端口用于传递数值或逻辑；电气、机械、热和流体物理端口参与网络方程。不能把物理端口当作普通输入输出，也不能用一个布尔保护请求代替实际的能量或物质通路。Causal signal connectors convey values or logic. Electrical, mechanical, thermal and fluid connectors participate in network equations. A physical connector is not an ordinary signal input, and a Boolean request does not replace an energy or material path.
03

按研究问题选择元件Select components by the research question

功能分区Functional area定义数Definitions什么时候用、怎样接入When and how to use it
Signals
信号预处理Signal preparation		6为相同单位、对齐的信号构造差值、绝对误差、相对误差或环绕角误差。有限差分须明确采样周期；相对误差要处理零或过小分母。预处理后的信号再送给统计或需求模块。Prepare differences, absolute/relative error or wrapped angular error from aligned, unit-compatible signals. Declare finite-difference sampling and handle small denominators before passing the result to statistics or requirements.
Statistics
连续与采样统计Continuous and sampled statistics		15按问题选择 Integral、TimeMean/TimeRMS/TimeVariance 或 Sample 类；统一窗口与资格规则，并把 value 与 available/dataStatus 一起观察。积分单位包含时间，均值与输入同单位，方差为单位平方。Choose integration, time-weighted or sampled statistics according to the question. Align window and validity rules and inspect value together with availability/status. Integral units include time, means retain input units and variance has squared units.
Response
动态响应与事件Dynamic response and events		17连接被观测响应或 request/response，声明参考值、阈值、stepTime、deadline、dwell 和采样规则。SettlingTime 要到完整窗口末端才能确认最终入带；事件类须检查计数、队列容量与删失。Connect the observed response or request/response pair and declare references, levels, step time, deadline, dwell and sampling. SettlingTime confirms final band entry only at a complete window end. Inspect event counts, queue capacity and censoring.
Requirements
显式需求判定Explicit requirement assessment		14先写清上界、下界、带内、持续违规或截止时间要求，再设置对应参数。接入真实资格信号时启用 useValidInput；不要把未观测完但没有发现违规直接写成 SATISFIED。Define an upper/lower bound, band, persistence or deadline requirement before setting parameters. Enable useValidInput for actual qualification. Do not report an unfinished observation with no witnessed violation as SATISFIED.
Comparison
配对比较与一致性Paired comparison and consistency		7连接语义可比的结果；单位、窗口、指标定义和优选方向由研究者确认。Improvement 的相对值是比例；参考值过小时需看 relativeAvailable。标签统计与多通道数值一致性是不同问题。Connect semantically comparable results and verify units, windows, metric definitions and preferred direction. Relative improvement is a fraction and requires relativeAvailable for small baselines. Classification statistics differ from numeric channel consistency.
Frequency
有限窗口频域分析Finite-window frequency analysis		5明确 samplePeriod、nSamples、频率与窗设置，检查窗口能否收集完整样本。单频、谐波畸变、双信号增益相位和峰值衰减各有适用条件；不能把未填满的缓冲或任意非平稳片段解释为可靠频谱。Declare sampling, sample count, frequencies and window settings and ensure full acquisition. Single-tone measures, distortion, two-signal gain/phase and peak decay have distinct assumptions. An unfilled buffer or arbitrary non-stationary segment is not a qualified spectrum.
Results
结果组织与输出Result organisation and output		6把评价器 result 接到 u[n]，配置各行标识、单位、窗口和需求版本。选择 CSVWriter、TextWriter 或 ConsoleWriter，outputDirectory 须已存在；默认不覆盖已有文件。PartialWriter 是抽象基类，不能单独实例化。Connect evaluator result to u[n] and configure row identifiers, units, windows and requirement versions. Select a CSV, text or console writer. outputDirectory must exist and existing files are not overwritten by default. PartialWriter is a partial base, not a stand-alone component.

下表与后文目录只使用本次源码中实际存在的定义。模型目录 CSV 的验证标签是仓库已有元数据，不代表本指南重新完成了运行验证。The package map and reference include definitions actually present in the reviewed source. Validation labels in the repository CSV are existing metadata, not new execution evidence supplied by this guide.

04

从加载到看懂第一组结果From loading the library to interpreting the first result

准备 Modelica Standard Library 4.0.0 和能够加载该版本的 Modelica 工具。保留源码目录结构；打开根目录 package.mo，而不是逐个加载底层 .mo 文件。克隆目录名称宜与根包名称一致。Prepare Modelica Standard Library 4.0.0 and a Modelica tool that can load that version. Preserve the source directory structure and open the root package.mo rather than loading leaf files separately. Prefer a checkout directory named after the root package.

git clone https://github.com/Ruizhe-Yang/EvaluationLibrary.git EvaluationLibrary
cd EvaluationLibrary
git checkout 18fb8c3ca0bf177ad0ba41ce978059170e0f4938
顺序Step图形工具中的操作Action in a graphical tool
1加载 MSL 4.0.0，再加载本库根 package.mo；检查消息窗口。Load MSL 4.0.0, then the root package.mo; inspect loading diagnostics.
2打开 EvaluationLibrary.Examples.AnalyticStatistics，先查看 Diagram 和参数，再进入仿真设置。Open EvaluationLibrary.Examples.AnalyticStatistics; inspect the Diagram and parameters before simulation settings.
3采用下方源码声明的实验设置。运行后先检查求解器是否正常终止，不要仅凭出现结果文件判断成功。Use the experiment settings declared below. Check successful solver termination before interpreting any result file.
4绘制示例卡指定的输入、输出及资格状态；在事件前、事件中、事件后分别读值。Plot the input, output and qualification variables listed in the example card; inspect values before, during and after each event.
// Run from the repository root after MSL 4.0.0 is installed.
loadModel(Modelica, {"4.0.0"});
loadFile("package.mo");
getErrorString();
checkModel(EvaluationLibrary.Examples.AnalyticStatistics);
simulate(EvaluationLibrary.Examples.AnalyticStatistics,
  startTime=0, stopTime=2.1, numberOfIntervals=1000, tolerance=1e-6);
getErrorString();
脚本将输出网格统一设为 1000 个区间，仅便于第一次观察。元件内部 samplePeriod、通信服务网格或离散事件逻辑不会因此自动改变；后文同时列出各示例原有的 Interval。The script uses 1,000 output intervals for initial inspection. This does not change an internal samplePeriod, communication service grid or discrete-event policy. The original Interval annotation is also shown for each example.

直接引用此类的示例：Examples directly referencing this class: ConsistencyAndLabels

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block ChannelConsistency "Compare the range of qualified simultaneous channels"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Integer n(min=2)=3;
  parameter Boolean useChannelValid=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u[n] ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput channelValid[n] if useChannelValid ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput nValid ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput validity[n] ;
  Boolean eligible[n];
initial equation
  assert(n>=2,"At least two channels are required.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and nValid>=2;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(channelValid,validity);
  if not useChannelValid then validity=fill(true,n); end if;
  for i in 1:n loop
    eligible[i]=validity[i] and abs(u[i])<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  end for;
  nValid=sum({if eligible[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n});
  available=gate.inWindow and dataOK;
  value=if available then max({if eligible[i] then u[i] else -Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n})
                       -min({if eligible[i] then u[i] else Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n}) else 0;
end ChannelConsistency;

直接引用此类的示例：Examples directly referencing this class: PairedPerformance

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block Improvement "Report absolute and relative improvement with an explicit preferred direction"
extends EvaluationLibrary.Comparison.PairedDifference;
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Sense sense=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Sense.LowerIsBetter;
  parameter Real referenceFloor(min=0)=1e-12;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput absoluteImprovement ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput relativeImprovement ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput relativeAvailable ;
initial equation
  assert(referenceFloor>=0,"Negative baseline floor.");
equation
  absoluteImprovement=if sense==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Sense.LowerIsBetter then -difference else difference;
  relativeAvailable=available and abs(reference.value)>referenceFloor;
  relativeImprovement=if relativeAvailable then absoluteImprovement/abs(reference.value) else 0;
end Improvement;
Comparison.LabelPerformance统计二分类样本及分母可用性Accumulate binary classification counts and denominator availabilityblock

在自己的实验模型中实例化此类，设置参数，连接所有启用的输入和所需物理参考，再对照示例运行。下列声明是实际源码接口；条件端口只在对应参数打开时存在。Instantiate this class in an experiment, set its parameters, connect every enabled input and required physical reference, then compare with an example. Accumulate sample-level binary classification counts and denominator flags

within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block LabelPerformance "Accumulate sample-level binary classification counts and denominator flags"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput truth ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput decision ;
  output Integer TP(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Integer TN(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Integer FP(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Integer FN(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Real accuracy;
  output Real recall;
  output Real precision;
  output Real specificity;
  output Boolean accuracyAvailable;
  output Boolean recallAvailable;
  output Boolean precisionAvailable;
  output Boolean specificityAvailable;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;

equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  accuracyAvailable=TP+TN+FP+FN>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID;
  recallAvailable=TP+FN>0 and accuracyAvailable;
  precisionAvailable=TP+FP>0 and accuracyAvailable;
  specificityAvailable=TN+FP>0 and accuracyAvailable;
  accuracy=if accuracyAvailable then (TP+TN)/(TP+TN+FP+FN) else 0;
  recall=if recallAvailable then TP/(TP+FN) else 0;
  precision=if precisionAvailable then TP/(TP+FP) else 0;
  specificity=if specificityAvailable then TN/(TN+FP) else 0;
  value=accuracy;
  available=accuracyAvailable;
algorithm

  when gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK then
    TP:=pre(TP)+(if truth and decision then 1 else 0);
    TN:=pre(TN)+(if not truth and not decision then 1 else 0);
    FP:=pre(FP)+(if not truth and decision then 1 else 0);
    FN:=pre(FN)+(if truth and not decision then 1 else 0);
  end when;
end LabelPerformance;

within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block MultiRunSummary "Summarize explicitly connected complete and comparable runs"
parameter Integer n(min=1)=3;
  parameter String comparisonId="comparison";
  parameter String comparisonIds[n]=fill(comparisonId,n);
  parameter String unit="1";
  parameter String units[n]=fill(unit,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time runStart[n]=fill(windowStart,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time runStop[n]=fill(windowStop,n);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput u[n] ;
  output Integer nInputs;
  output Integer nValid;
  output Boolean available;
  output Real minimum;
  output Real maximum;
  output Real mean;
protected
  Boolean accepted[n];
initial equation
  assert(n>=1 and comparisonId<>"" and windowStop>windowStart,"Invalid comparison metadata.");
equation
  for i in 1:n loop
    accepted[i]=comparisonIds[i]==comparisonId and units[i]==unit and abs(runStart[i]-windowStart)<=1e-9
      and abs(runStop[i]-windowStop)<=1e-9 and u[i].complete and u[i].dataStatus==2 and u[i].valueAvailable
      and abs(u[i].value)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  end for;
  nInputs=n;
  nValid=sum({if accepted[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n});
  available=nValid>0;
  minimum=if available then min({if accepted[i] then u[i].value else Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n}) else 0;
  maximum=if available then max({if accepted[i] then u[i].value else -Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n}) else 0;
  mean=if available then sum({if accepted[i] then u[i].value else 0 for i in 1:n})/nValid else 0;
end MultiRunSummary;

within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block PairedArrivalSkew "双事件首达时差及时间一致性"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time maximumSkew(min=0) = 0.1 "Maximum absolute skew";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput a "First stream arrival condition" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput b "Second stream arrival condition" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput skew(unit="s") "First B time minus first A time" ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time arrivedA(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time arrivedB(start=0,fixed=true);
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  discrete Boolean seenA(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean seenB(start=false,fixed=true);
  Boolean hitA;
  Boolean hitB;
initial equation
  assert(maximumSkew >= 0,"Skew must be nonnegative.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid;
  hitA = inWindow and dataOK and a;
  hitB = inWindow and dataOK and b;
  when {initial(),hitA} then
    seenA = pre(seenA) or hitA;
    arrivedA = if hitA and not pre(seenA) then time else pre(arrivedA);
  end when;
  when {initial(),hitB} then
    seenB = pre(seenB) or hitB;
    arrivedB = if hitB and not pre(seenB) then time else pre(arrivedB);
  end when;
  skew = if seenA and seenB then arrivedB-arrivedA else 0;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if not (seenA and seenB) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED elseif noEvent(abs(skew)<=maximumSkew) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end PairedArrivalSkew;

within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block PairedDifference "Compare two qualified results with an explicitly matched interpretation"
parameter String comparisonId="comparison";
  parameter String referenceComparisonId=comparisonId;
  parameter String candidateComparisonId=comparisonId;
  parameter String referenceUnit="1";
  parameter String candidateUnit=referenceUnit;
  parameter Real referenceStart=0;
  parameter Real candidateStart=referenceStart;
  parameter Real referenceStop=5;
  parameter Real candidateStop=referenceStop;
  parameter Real timeTolerance(min=0)=1e-9;

  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput reference ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput candidate ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput difference ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  output Boolean metadataMatch;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
initial equation
  assert(timeTolerance>=0 and referenceStop>referenceStart and candidateStop>candidateStart,"Invalid comparison windows or tolerance.");
equation
  metadataMatch=comparisonId<>"" and referenceComparisonId==comparisonId and candidateComparisonId==comparisonId
    and referenceUnit==candidateUnit and abs(referenceStart-candidateStart)<=timeTolerance
    and abs(referenceStop-candidateStop)<=timeTolerance;
  result.complete=reference.complete and candidate.complete;
  dataStatus=if not metadataMatch or reference.dataStatus==4 or candidate.dataStatus==4 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif reference.dataStatus==5 or candidate.dataStatus==5 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif not result.complete then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING
    elseif reference.dataStatus==2 and candidate.dataStatus==2 and reference.valueAvailable and candidate.valueAvailable then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE
    else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE;
  available=dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE;
  difference=if available then candidate.value-reference.value else 0;
  result.value=difference;
  result.valueAvailable=available;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,false,false));
  result.everViolated=false;
  result.firstViolationTime=0;
  result.validDuration=min(reference.validDuration,candidate.validDuration);
  result.expectedSamples=0;
  result.receivedSamples=0;
  result.validSamples=0;
end PairedDifference;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Comparison;
block SampleCorrelation "双信号的中心化样本相关系数"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput x "First signal" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput y "Second signal" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput correlation(min=-1,max=1) "Pearson correlation on the observed samples" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput defined "Both signals have nonzero variance and valid observations" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  discrete Real meanX(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real meanY(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real squareX(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real squareY(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real covariance(start=0,fixed=true);
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(x) < Modelica.Constants.inf) and noEvent(abs(y) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    if takeTick and dataOK then
      meanX = pre(meanX)+(x-pre(meanX))/max(1,validSamples);
      meanY = pre(meanY)+(y-pre(meanY))/max(1,validSamples);
      squareX = pre(squareX)+(x-pre(meanX))*(x-meanX);
      squareY = pre(squareY)+(y-pre(meanY))*(y-meanY);
      covariance = pre(covariance)+(x-pre(meanX))*(y-meanY);
    else
      meanX = pre(meanX);
      meanY = pre(meanY);
      squareX = pre(squareX);
      squareY = pre(squareY);
      covariance = pre(covariance);
    end if;
  end when;
  defined = validSamples >= 2 and squareX > 0 and squareY > 0 and dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID;
  correlation = if defined then min(1,max(-1,(covariance/sqrt(squareX))/sqrt(squareY))) else 0;
end SampleCorrelation;

within EvaluationLibrary.Frequency;
block DecayRatio "Measure attenuation across qualified positive sampled peaks"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real peakFloor(min=0)=1e-8;
  parameter Real growthTolerance(min=0)=1e-5;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Integer peakCount(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Real ratio;
  output Real logarithmicDecrement;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  discrete Real secondLast(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real last(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Integer seen(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real previousPeak(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real ratioSum(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real logSum(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean grew(start=false,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(peakFloor>0 and growthTolerance>=0,"Invalid peak thresholds.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=grew;
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  available=peakCount>=3 and not grew and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  ratio=if available then ratioSum/(peakCount-1) else 0;
  logarithmicDecrement=if available then logSum/(peakCount-1) else 0;
  value=ratio;
algorithm

  when gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK then
    if seen>=2 and last>peakFloor and last>secondLast and last>=u then
      if peakCount>0 then
        grew:=pre(grew) or last>previousPeak*(1+growthTolerance);
        ratioSum:=pre(ratioSum)+last/previousPeak;
        logSum:=pre(logSum)+log(previousPeak/last);
      end if;
      peakCount:=pre(peakCount)+1;
      previousPeak:=last;
    end if;
    secondLast:=last;
    last:=u;
    seen:=pre(seen)+1;
  end when;
end DecayRatio;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;

  parameter Boolean removeMean=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency frequencyResolution;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency nyquist;

  final parameter Integer nBins=div(nSamples,2)+1;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency frequency[nBins];
  output Real amplitude[nBins](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SpectrumBuffer cache(n=nSamples);
  discrete Real re(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real im(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real phase(start=0,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(nSamples>=16 and nSamples<=1024,"Invalid spectral sample count.");

equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=cache.overflow or (false);
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=if gate.status==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and cache.count<>nSamples then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE else gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  cache.u=u;
  cache.take=gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK and gate.tick;
  frequencyResolution=1/(nSamples*samplePeriod);
  nyquist=1/(2*samplePeriod);

  for k in 1:nBins loop frequency[k]=(k-1)*frequencyResolution; end for;
  value=if available then max(amplitude) else 0;
  available=cache.ready and gate.complete and dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and (true);
algorithm
  when cache.ready then
    for k in 1:nBins loop
      (re,im,amplitude[k],phase):=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.Tone(cache.samples,samplePeriod,frequency[k],taper,removeMean);
    end for;
  end when;
end DiscreteSpectrum;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;

  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency nyquist;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency fundamental(min=0)=5;
  parameter Integer harmonics(min=2)=4;
  parameter Real amplitudeFloor(min=0)=1e-8;
  output Real amplitude[harmonics](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  output Real thd;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SpectrumBuffer cache(n=nSamples);
  discrete Real re(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real im(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real phase(start=0,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(nSamples>=16 and nSamples<=1024,"Invalid spectral sample count.");
  assert(fundamental>0 and harmonics>=2 and harmonics*fundamental*samplePeriod<0.5 and amplitudeFloor>=0,"A harmonic is outside the allowed range.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=cache.overflow or (cache.ready and gate.complete and amplitude[1]<=amplitudeFloor);
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=if gate.status==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and cache.count<>nSamples then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE else gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  cache.u=u;
  cache.take=gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK and gate.tick;
  frequencyResolution=1/(nSamples*samplePeriod);
  nyquist=1/(2*samplePeriod);

  thd=if available then sqrt(sum({amplitude[h]^2 for h in 2:harmonics}))/amplitude[1] else 0;
  value=thd;
  available=cache.ready and gate.complete and dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and (amplitude[1]>amplitudeFloor);
algorithm
  when cache.ready then
    for h in 1:harmonics loop
      (re,im,amplitude[h],phase):=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.Tone(cache.samples,samplePeriod,h*fundamental,taper,removeMean);
    end for;
  end when;
end HarmonicDistortion;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Frequency;
block SingleFrequency "Evaluate the amplitude and phase of one selected frequency"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  parameter Integer nSamples(min=16,max=1024)=200;
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper taper=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper.Rectangular;
  parameter Boolean removeMean=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency frequencyResolution;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency nyquist;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency frequency(min=0)=5;
  output Real re(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Real im(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Real amplitude(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Real phase(unit="rad",start=0,fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SpectrumBuffer cache(n=nSamples);

initial equation
  assert(nSamples>=16 and nSamples<=1024,"Invalid spectral sample count.");
  assert(frequency>=0 and frequency*samplePeriod<=0.5,"The selected frequency exceeds Nyquist.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=cache.overflow or (false);
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=if gate.status==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and cache.count<>nSamples then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE else gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  cache.u=u;
  cache.take=gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK and gate.tick;
  frequencyResolution=1/(nSamples*samplePeriod);
  nyquist=1/(2*samplePeriod);
  value=if available then amplitude else 0;
  available=cache.ready and gate.complete and dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and (true);
algorithm
  when cache.ready then
    (re,im,amplitude,phase):=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.Tone(cache.samples,samplePeriod,frequency,taper,removeMean);
  end when;
end SingleFrequency;

within EvaluationLibrary.Frequency;
block ToneResponse "以共同采样窗估计单频增益和相位差"
  parameter Integer nSamples(min=4,max=1024) = 200;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time startTime = 0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.005;
  parameter Integer bin(min=1,max=div(nSamples-1,2)) = min(8,div(nSamples-1,2));
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow window = EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow.Rectangular;
  parameter Real minInputAmplitude(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1e-6 "Amplitude floor, input units";
  parameter Real minOutputAmplitude(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1e-6 "Phase amplitude floor, output units";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput excitation ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput response ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Common validity flag" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput gain "Output amplitude / input amplitude" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput phaseDifference(unit="rad") "Output minus input phase in [-pi,pi]" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput gainDefined ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput phaseDefined ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput frequency(unit="Hz") ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ToneSpectrum x(nSamples=nSamples,nBins=bin,startTime=startTime,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,window=window,amplitudeFloor=minInputAmplitude);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ToneSpectrum y(nSamples=nSamples,nBins=bin,startTime=startTime,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,window=window,amplitudeFloor=minOutputAmplitude);
  Real delta(unit="rad");
initial equation
  assert(bin>=1 and 2*bin<nSamples and minInputAmplitude>0 and minOutputAmplitude>0, "A non-Nyquist positive bin and positive floors are required.");
equation
  x.u = excitation;
  y.u = response;
  x.valid = valid;
  y.valid = valid;
  dataState = if x.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID or y.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif x.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and y.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE
    elseif x.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE or y.dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING;
  gainDefined = dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and x.amplitude[bin+1]>minInputAmplitude;
  phaseDefined = gainDefined and y.amplitude[bin+1]>minOutputAmplitude;
  gain = if gainDefined then y.amplitude[bin+1]/x.amplitude[bin+1] else 0;
  delta = y.phase[bin+1]-x.phase[bin+1];
  phaseDifference = if phaseDefined then Modelica.Math.atan2(sin(delta),cos(delta)) else 0;
  frequency = bin*x.resolution;
end ToneResponse;

block BooleanRequirement "Require a qualified Boolean value throughout the window"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  parameter Boolean expectedValue=true;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;

equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (u<>expectedValue);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;
  value=if everViolated then 0 else 1;
  available=gate.validDuration>0 or everViolated;
end BooleanRequirement;

按最短圆周弧判断角度跟踪误差

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block CircularErrorBound "按最短圆周弧判断角度跟踪误差"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput violationObserved "窗口中至少一次观察到越限" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput firstViolationTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "首次观察到越限的时刻" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "规则判定";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Angle bound(min=0,max=Modelica.Constants.pi) = 0.1 "误差幅值上界，rad";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput angle(unit="rad") "实测角" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference(unit="rad") "参考角" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput error(unit="rad") "有符号最短角误差" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Boolean outside "派生资产定义的越限条件";
  Boolean violationNow;
  discrete Boolean seen(start=false,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(bound >= 0 and bound <= Modelica.Constants.pi, "Circular bound must be in [0,pi].");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  violationNow = inWindow and dataOK and outside;
  when {initial(), violationNow} then
    seen = if initial() then violationNow else true;
    firstViolationTime = if violationNow and not pre(seen) then time else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  violationObserved = seen;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if seen then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED;
  dataOK = valid and abs(angle) < Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(reference) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  error = if dataOK then Modelica.Math.atan2(sin(angle-reference),cos(angle-reference)) else 0;
  outside = abs(error) > bound;
end CircularErrorBound;

Combine required child verdicts with explicit qualification priority

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block Conjunction "Combine required child verdicts with explicit qualification priority"
parameter Integer n(min=1)=3;
  parameter Boolean required[n]=fill(true,n);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput u[n] ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput child[n] ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
protected
  Real witnesses[n];
  Boolean requiredActive[n];
initial equation
  assert(n>=1,"At least one result is required.");
equation
  for i in 1:n loop
    child[i].value=u[i].value;
    child[i].valueAvailable=u[i].valueAvailable;
    child[i].dataStatus=u[i].dataStatus;
    child[i].verdict=u[i].verdict;
    child[i].everViolated=u[i].everViolated;
    child[i].firstViolationTime=u[i].firstViolationTime;
    child[i].validDuration=u[i].validDuration;
    child[i].expectedSamples=u[i].expectedSamples;
    child[i].receivedSamples=u[i].receivedSamples;
    child[i].validSamples=u[i].validSamples;
    child[i].complete=u[i].complete;
    requiredActive[i]=required[i] and u[i].dataStatus<>Integer(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE);
    witnesses[i]=if requiredActive[i] and u[i].everViolated then u[i].firstViolationTime else Modelica.Constants.inf;
  end for;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.Aggregate({u[i].verdict for i in 1:n},required);
  everViolated=min(witnesses)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  firstViolationTime=if everViolated then min(witnesses) else 0;
  result.complete=sum({if requiredActive[i] and not u[i].complete then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})==0;
  dataStatus=if sum({if requiredActive[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})==0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif sum({if requiredActive[i] and u[i].dataStatus==4 then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif not result.complete then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING
    elseif sum({if requiredActive[i] and u[i].dataStatus<>2 then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE
    else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE;
  result.value=0;
  result.valueAvailable=false;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=if dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then 0 else min({if requiredActive[i] then u[i].validDuration else Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n});
  result.expectedSamples=0;
  result.receivedSamples=0;
  result.validSamples=0;
end Conjunction;

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block LabelEpisodeDelay "评价首个真值事件内的检出时差"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "窗口开始，s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "窗口结束，s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time deadline(min=0) = 0.5 "最大检出时间，s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "本次评价适用";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "真值和检测标签同时有效" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput truth "独立真值事件" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput detected "系统报告检测" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput latency(unit="s") "首个事件的检测时差" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput detectedInEpisode "首个事件已检出" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "证据状态";
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "按截止时间和事件结束证据判定";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Requirements.ResponseTime timer(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,deadline=deadline,applicable=applicable);
  discrete Boolean ended(start=false,fixed=true);
  Boolean firstEpisodeEnded;
equation
  timer.valid = valid;
  timer.request = truth;
  timer.response = truth and detected and not ended;
  firstEpisodeEnded = timer.triggered and not truth and timer.inWindow and valid;
  when {initial(),firstEpisodeEnded} then
    ended = if initial() then false else true;
  end when;
  latency = timer.latency;
  detectedInEpisode = timer.responded;
  dataState = timer.dataState;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and ended and not timer.responded then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED else timer.verdict;
end LabelEpisodeDelay;

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block LowerBound "Evaluate a signed lower bound"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real lower=0;
  parameter Real tolerance(min=0)=0;
  parameter Boolean sampled=false "Use only qualified samples on the declared sample grid";
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Real margin;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=sampled,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean qualifiedTick;
  Boolean sampleFailed;
initial equation
  assert(tolerance>=0 and tolerance<Modelica.Constants.inf and (abs(lower)<Modelica.Constants.inf) and (true),"Invalid bound or tolerance.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if sampled then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (if sampled then sampleFailed else margin<0);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  margin=u-lower+tolerance;
  qualifiedTick=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  sampleFailed=qualifiedTick and margin<0;
  available=if sampled then gate.validSamples>0 else gate.validDuration>0 or everViolated;
  value=if available and not everViolated then 1 else 0;
end LowerBound;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block NumericRequirement "按完整数值证据执行含等号的限值要求"
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.NumericComparison comparison = EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.NumericComparison.Inside;
  parameter Real lower = 0 "Inclusive lower limit";
  parameter Real upper = 1 "Inclusive upper limit";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput value "Numerical evidence in the units of the bounds" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput defined "The metric value exists" ;
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Evidence coverage and validity";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput margin "Signed distance to applicable boundary" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "Requirement outcome";
protected
  Boolean finiteValue;
initial equation
  assert(comparison <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.NumericComparison.Inside or lower <= upper, "Inverted range.");
equation
  finiteValue = noEvent(abs(value) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  margin = if defined and finiteValue then
    (if comparison == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.NumericComparison.AtMost then upper-value elseif comparison == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.NumericComparison.AtLeast then value-lower
      else min(value-lower,upper-value)) else 0;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if not defined then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED elseif not finiteValue then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID elseif noEvent(margin>=0) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end NumericRequirement;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block ObjectiveCompletion "按独立目标证据和否决条件判定任务完成"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput objectiveMet "任务目标证据" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput failure "否决条件" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput completed(start=false,fixed=true) "窗口内已观察目标" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput vetoed(start=false,fixed=true) "窗口内已观察否决" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput completionTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "首个目标证据时刻" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "完整窗口的任务结果";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Boolean hit;
  Boolean veto;
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid;
  hit = inWindow and dataOK and objectiveMet;
  veto = inWindow and dataOK and failure;
  when {initial(),hit} then
    completed = if initial() then hit else true;
    completionTime = if hit and not pre(completed) then time else pre(completionTime);
  end when;
  when {initial(),veto} then
    vetoed = if initial() then veto else true;
  end when;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if completed and not vetoed then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end ObjectiveCompletion;

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block PersistenceLimit "Detect a single qualified continuous violation beyond a duration"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time maxDuration(min=0)=1;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput u ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time currentDuration;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time longestDuration;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean during;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time began(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time longestComplete(start=0,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(maxDuration>=0,"Negative persistence duration.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (during and currentDuration>maxDuration);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  during=gate.inWindow and dataOK and u;
  when {initial(),change(during)} then
    began=if initial() or during then time else pre(began);
    longestComplete=if initial() then 0 elseif not during then max(pre(longestComplete),time-pre(began)) else pre(longestComplete);
  end when;
  currentDuration=if during then time-began else 0;
  longestDuration=max(longestComplete,currentDuration);
  value=longestDuration;
  available=gate.validDuration>0;
end PersistenceLimit;

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block ResponseTime "测量首次请求至实际响应并按截止时间判定"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time deadline(min=0) = 0.5 "允许最大响应时差，s";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput request "请求或事件条件" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput response "来自实测的动作或恢复条件" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput triggered(start=false,fixed=true) "已记录请求" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput responded(start=false,fixed=true) "已观察响应" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput triggerTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "首个请求时刻" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput responseAt(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "首个响应时刻" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput latency(unit="s") "有响应证据时的时差" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "按期限的结果";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Boolean triggerNow;
  Boolean responseNow;
initial equation
  assert(deadline >= 0, "Deadline must be nonnegative.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid;
  triggerNow = inWindow and dataOK and request;
  responseNow = inWindow and dataOK and triggered and response;
  when {initial(),triggerNow} then
    triggered = if initial() then triggerNow else true;
    triggerTime = if triggerNow and not pre(triggered) then time else pre(triggerTime);
  end when;
  when {initial(),responseNow} then
    responded = if initial() then responseNow else true;
    responseAt = if responseNow and not pre(responded) then time else pre(responseAt);
  end when;
  latency = if responded then responseAt-triggerTime else 0;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if not triggered then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE elseif responded then (if noEvent(latency <= deadline) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED) elseif noEvent(triggerTime+deadline < windowEnd) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED;
end ResponseTime;

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block SignedMargin "连续累计相对安全裕量的缺口"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Real normalization(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1 "裕量归一化尺度，输入单位";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput available "实际可用量" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput required "任务要求" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput margin "瞬时归一化裕量" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput deficitArea(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "负裕量幅值的时间积分" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
initial equation
  assert(normalization > 0 and abs(normalization) < Modelica.Constants.inf, "Normalization must be finite and positive.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and abs(available) < Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(required) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  margin = if dataOK then (available-required)/normalization else 0;
  der(deficitArea) = if inWindow and dataOK then max(0,-margin) else 0;
end SignedMargin;
