实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block UpperBound "Evaluate a signed upper bound"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real upper=1;
  parameter Real tolerance(min=0)=0;
  parameter Boolean sampled=false "Use only qualified samples on the declared sample grid";
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Real margin;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=sampled,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean qualifiedTick;
  Boolean sampleFailed;
initial equation
  assert(tolerance>=0 and tolerance<Modelica.Constants.inf and (abs(upper)<Modelica.Constants.inf) and (true),"Invalid bound or tolerance.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if sampled then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (if sampled then sampleFailed else margin<0);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  margin=upper+tolerance-u;
  qualifiedTick=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  sampleFailed=qualifiedTick and margin<0;
  available=if sampled then gate.validSamples>0 else gate.validDuration>0 or everViolated;
  value=if available and not everViolated then 1 else 0;
end UpperBound;

parameter Real upper[n]=fill(1,n);

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block VectorBounds "Evaluate independent finite numeric channel bounds"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated(start=false,fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime(start=0,fixed=true);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Integer n(min=1)=3;
  parameter Real lower[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter Real upper[n]=fill(1,n);
  parameter Real tolerance[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter String channelUnit[n]=fill("1",n);
  parameter Boolean useChannelValid=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u[n] ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput channelValid[n] if useChannelValid ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput perChannelVerdict[n] ;
  output Boolean channelViolated[n](each start=false,each fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time channelFirstViolation[n](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput validity[n] ;
  Boolean qualifiedChannel[n];
  Boolean violation[n];
  Boolean invalid[n];
  discrete Boolean invalidSeen[n](each start=false,each fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(n>=1,"At least one channel is required.");
  for i in 1:n loop
    assert(lower[i]<=upper[i] and tolerance[i]>=0 and abs(lower[i])<Modelica.Constants.inf and
      abs(upper[i])<Modelica.Constants.inf and tolerance[i]<Modelica.Constants.inf,"Invalid channel bound.");
  end for;
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=sum({if qualifiedChannel[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})==n;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(channelValid,validity);
  if not useChannelValid then validity=fill(true,n); end if;
  for i in 1:n loop
    qualifiedChannel[i]=trusted and validity[i] and abs(u[i])<Modelica.Constants.inf;
    violation[i]=gate.inWindow and qualifiedChannel[i] and (u[i]<lower[i]-tolerance[i] or u[i]>upper[i]+tolerance[i]);
    invalid[i]=gate.inWindow and not qualifiedChannel[i];
    when {initial(),violation[i],invalid[i]} then
      channelViolated[i]=(not initial() and pre(channelViolated[i])) or violation[i];
      channelFirstViolation[i]=if violation[i] and (initial() or not pre(channelViolated[i])) then time
        elseif initial() then 0 else pre(channelFirstViolation[i]);
      invalidSeen[i]=(not initial() and pre(invalidSeen[i])) or invalid[i];
    end when;
    perChannelVerdict[i]=Integer(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(if invalidSeen[i] then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
      elseif gate.status==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then (if gate.complete and gate.actualStop>gate.actualStart and
      gate.observedDuration>=gate.actualStop-gate.actualStart-1e-7 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE)
      else gate.status,channelViolated[i],true));
  end for;
  value=sum({if channelViolated[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n});
  available=gate.observedDuration>0 or everViolated;
algorithm
  when {initial(),sum({if violation[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0} then
    if sum({if violation[i] then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0 and not pre(everViolated) then
      everViolated:=true;
      firstViolationTime:=time;
    end if;
  end when;
end VectorBounds;

输入、输出和物理端口（12 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (12)

within EvaluationLibrary.Requirements;
block WithinBand "Evaluate inclusive upper and lower bounds"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real lower=0;
  parameter Real upper=1;
  parameter Real tolerance(min=0)=0;
  parameter Boolean sampled=false "Use only qualified samples on the declared sample grid";
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  output Real margin;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=sampled,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean qualifiedTick;
  Boolean sampleFailed;
initial equation
  assert(tolerance>=0 and tolerance<Modelica.Constants.inf and (abs(lower)<Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(upper)<Modelica.Constants.inf) and (lower<=upper),"Invalid bound or tolerance.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if sampled then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (if sampled then sampleFailed else margin<0);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,true);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  margin=min(u-lower+tolerance,upper+tolerance-u);
  qualifiedTick=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  sampleFailed=qualifiedTick and margin<0;
  available=if sampled then gate.validSamples>0 else gate.validDuration>0 or everViolated;
  value=if available and not everViolated then 1 else 0;
end WithinBand;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block ConditionOccupancy "量化危险状态占用比例并按用户预算判定"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Real budget(min=0,max=1) = 0.05 "允许占用比例";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput unsafe "真实危险状态" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput duration(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "危险状态累计时长" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput fraction "危险时长占有效时长之比" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "占用预算判定";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
initial equation
  assert(budget >= 0 and budget <= 1, "Occupancy budget must be in [0,1].");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid;
  der(duration) = if inWindow and dataOK and unsafe then 1 else 0;
  fraction = if noEvent(observedDuration > 0) then min(1,max(0,duration/observedDuration)) else 0;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if fraction <= budget then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end ConditionOccupancy;

相邻事件间隔及最短观测间隔

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block EventIntervals "相邻事件间隔及最短观测间隔"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput eventSignal "An event is a rising edge" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput count(start=0,fixed=true) "Events observed in the window" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput lastInterval(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "Most recent complete interval" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput minInterval(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "Shortest complete interval" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput intervalDefined "At least two events have occurred" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time lastTime(start=0,fixed=true);
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid;
  when {initial(),eventSignal} then
    if not initial() and inWindow and dataOK then
      count = pre(count)+1;
      lastInterval = if pre(count)>0 then time-pre(lastTime) else 0;
      minInterval = if pre(count)==1 then lastInterval elseif pre(count)>1 then min(pre(minInterval),lastInterval) else 0;
      lastTime = time;
    else
      count = pre(count);
      lastInterval = pre(lastInterval);
      minInterval = pre(minInterval);
      lastTime = pre(lastTime);
    end if;
  end when;
  intervalDefined = count > 1;
end EventIntervals;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block FallTime "Measure ordered 10 to 90 percent progress of a declared step"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real referenceStart=1;
  parameter Real referenceFinal=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time stepTime=0.5;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput found(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time t10(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time t90(start=0,fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Real progress;
  Boolean cross10;
  Boolean cross90;
  discrete Boolean found10(start=false,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(referenceFinal<referenceStart,"Step direction and nonzero amplitude are required.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  progress=(u-referenceStart)/(referenceFinal-referenceStart);
  cross10=gate.inWindow and dataOK and time>=stepTime and progress>=0.1;
  cross90=gate.inWindow and dataOK and time>=stepTime and progress>=0.9;
  value=if found then t90-t10 else 0;
  available=found;
algorithm

  when {initial(),cross10,cross90} then
    if cross10 and not found10 then found10:=true; t10:=time; end if;
    if cross90 and found10 and not found then found:=true; t90:=time; end if;
  end when;
end FallTime;

parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time coverageTolerance(min=0) = 1e-7 "Numerical integration tolerance, s";
输入、输出和物理端口（5 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (5)
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Signal";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Input validity";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput mean "Trailing average, same unit as u";

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block MovingAverage "连续有效的完整滑窗平均值"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time length(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1 "Trailing window duration, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time coverageTolerance(min=0) = 1e-7 "Numerical integration tolerance, s";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Signal" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Input validity" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput mean "Trailing average, same unit as u" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput ready "A full valid duration is available" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput coveredDuration(unit="s") "Valid duration within the current window" ;
protected
  Real integral(start=0,fixed=true);
  Real duration(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time startTime(fixed=false);
  Boolean dataOK;
initial equation
  startTime = time;
  assert(length > 0 and coverageTolerance >= 0 and coverageTolerance < length, "Invalid moving window.");
equation
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  der(integral) = if dataOK then u else 0;
  der(duration) = if dataOK then 1 else 0;
  coveredDuration = min(length,max(0,duration-delay(duration,length)));
  ready = dataOK and time >= startTime+length and noEvent(coveredDuration >= length-coverageTolerance);
  mean = if ready then (integral-delay(integral,length))/length else 0;
end MovingAverage;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block OccurrenceCount "Count qualified rising events without counting sustained true samples"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Boolean countInitialTrue=true;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput count(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;

equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  value=count;
  available=gate.observedDuration>0 or count>0;
algorithm

  when {initial(),u,gate.inWindow} then
    if dataOK and gate.inWindow and u and
      ((countInitialTrue and (initial() or not pre(gate.inWindow))) or (not initial() and not pre(u))) then
      count:=pre(count)+1;
    end if;
  end when;
end OccurrenceCount;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block OvershootUndershoot "测量阶跃基准区间之外的归一化超调和反向欠调"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  parameter Real initialValue = 0 "Pre-step baseline";
  parameter Real target = 1 "Final target, different from initialValue";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Measured response" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput overshootPercent(unit="%") "Largest excursion past target" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput undershootPercent(unit="%") "Largest excursion opposite the step" ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time overshootAt(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time undershootAt(start=0,fixed=true);
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  discrete Real high(start=1,fixed=true);
  discrete Real low(start=0,fixed=true);
  Real normalized;
initial equation
  assert(abs(target-initialValue)>Modelica.Constants.small, "Target and initial value must differ.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  normalized = if dataOK then (u-initialValue)/(target-initialValue) else 0;
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    high = if takeTick and dataOK then max(pre(high),normalized) else pre(high);
    low = if takeTick and dataOK then min(pre(low),normalized) else pre(low);
    overshootAt = if takeTick and dataOK and normalized > pre(high) then time else pre(overshootAt);
    undershootAt = if takeTick and dataOK and normalized < pre(low) then time else pre(undershootAt);
  end when;
  overshootPercent = 100*(high-1);
  undershootPercent = -100*low;
end OvershootUndershoot;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block RecoveryTime "Confirm a qualified recovery dwell after the first danger event"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time recoveryDwell(min=0)=0.2;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput danger ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput inRecoveryBand ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput recovered(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time dangerTime(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time recoveredAt(start=0,fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean hazard;
  Boolean inDwell;
  Boolean qualifiedRecovery;
  discrete Boolean seenDanger(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time enteredAt(start=0,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(recoveryDwell>=0,"Negative recovery dwell.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  hazard=gate.inWindow and dataOK and danger;
  inDwell=gate.inWindow and dataOK and seenDanger and not danger and inRecoveryBand;
  qualifiedRecovery=inDwell and time>=enteredAt+recoveryDwell;
  when {initial(),inDwell} then enteredAt=time; end when;
  value=if recovered then recoveredAt-dangerTime else 0;
  available=recovered;
algorithm

  when {initial(),hazard,qualifiedRecovery} then
    if hazard and not seenDanger then seenDanger:=true; dangerTime:=time; end if;
    if qualifiedRecovery and not recovered then recovered:=true; recoveredAt:=time; end if;
  end when;
end RecoveryTime;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block RepeatedResponse "Pair repeated qualified events using a finite FIFO and explicit deadline"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time deadline(min=0)=0.2;
  parameter Boolean qualifyDeadline=false "Classify the result as a deadline requirement";
  parameter Integer capacity(min=1)=64;
  parameter Integer maxRecords(min=1)=128;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput request ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput response ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput matched(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput timedOut(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput censored(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput unmatchedResponse(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput overflow(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time delays[maxRecords](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  output Integer queueLength(start=0,fixed=true);

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;

  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time timestamps[capacity](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time delaySum(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstMissed(start=0,fixed=true);
  Boolean expired;

initial equation
  assert(deadline>=0 and capacity>=1 and maxRecords>=1,"Invalid queue dimensions or deadline.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=overflow;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  dataStatus=if qualifyDeadline and gate.status==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE and (censored>0 or matched+timedOut==0) then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE else gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,qualifyDeadline);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  expired=pre(queueLength)>0 and time>=pre(timestamps[1])+deadline;
  value=if matched>0 then delaySum/matched else 0;
  available=matched>0;
  everViolated=timedOut>0;
  firstViolationTime=firstMissed;

algorithm

  when {initial(),request,response,expired,gate.complete} then
    if initial() then queueLength:=0; end if;
    if gate.inWindow then
      while queueLength>0 and timestamps[1]+deadline<time-1e-10 loop
        if timedOut==0 then firstMissed:=timestamps[1]+deadline; end if;
        timedOut:=timedOut+1;
        for j in 1:queueLength-1 loop timestamps[j]:=timestamps[j+1]; end for;
        queueLength:=queueLength-1;
      end while;
      if dataOK and request and (initial() or not pre(request)) then
        if queueLength>=capacity then overflow:=true;
        else queueLength:=queueLength+1; timestamps[queueLength]:=time; end if;
      end if;
      if dataOK and response and (initial() or not pre(response)) then
        if queueLength<=0 then unmatchedResponse:=unmatchedResponse+1;
        elseif matched>=maxRecords then
          overflow:=true;
          for j in 1:queueLength-1 loop timestamps[j]:=timestamps[j+1]; end for;
          queueLength:=queueLength-1;
        else
          matched:=matched+1;
          delays[matched]:=time-timestamps[1];
          delaySum:=delaySum+delays[matched];
          for j in 1:queueLength-1 loop timestamps[j]:=timestamps[j+1]; end for;
          queueLength:=queueLength-1;
        end if;
      end if;
      while queueLength>0 and timestamps[1]+deadline<=time+1e-10 loop
        if timedOut==0 then firstMissed:=timestamps[1]+deadline; end if;
        timedOut:=timedOut+1;
        for j in 1:queueLength-1 loop timestamps[j]:=timestamps[j+1]; end for;
        queueLength:=queueLength-1;
      end while;
    elseif gate.complete then
      for j in 1:queueLength loop
        if timestamps[j]+deadline<gate.actualStop and timestamps[j]+deadline<=time then
          if timedOut==0 then firstMissed:=timestamps[j]+deadline; end if;
          timedOut:=timedOut+1;
        else censored:=censored+1; end if;
      end for;
      queueLength:=0;
    end if;
  end when;
end RepeatedResponse;

  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  value=if matched then responseTime-requestTime else 0;
  available=matched;
algorithm

  when {initial(),request,response} then
    if gate.inWindow and dataOK then
      if request and (initial() or not pre(request)) and not requested then
        requestTime:=time; requested:=true;
      end if;
      if response and (initial() or not pre(response)) and requested and not matched then
        responseTime:=time; matched:=true;
      end if;
    end if;
  end when;
end ResponseDelay;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block RiseFallTime "测量同向到达两个有序门限的时间差"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Real firstLevel = 0.1 "第一门限，输入单位";
  parameter Real secondLevel = 0.9 "第二门限，输入单位";
  parameter Boolean falling = false "取 true 时按下降方向解释";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "连续响应轨迹" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput started(start=false,fixed=true) "已达第一门限" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput finished(start=false,fixed=true) "已达第二门限" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput firstTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "第一门限时刻" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput secondTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "第二门限时刻" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput duration(unit="s") "时间差，未完成时填零" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Boolean firstHit;
  Boolean secondHit;
initial equation
  assert(if falling then firstLevel > secondLevel else firstLevel < secondLevel, "Levels must match the measurement direction.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  firstHit = inWindow and dataOK and (if falling then u <= firstLevel else u >= firstLevel);
  secondHit = inWindow and dataOK and started and (if falling then u <= secondLevel else u >= secondLevel);
  when {initial(), firstHit} then
    started = if initial() then firstHit else true;
    firstTime = if firstHit and not pre(started) then time else pre(firstTime);
  end when;
  when {initial(), secondHit} then
    finished = if initial() then secondHit else true;
    secondTime = if secondHit and not pre(finished) then time else pre(secondTime);
  end when;
  duration = if finished then secondTime-firstTime else 0;
end RiseFallTime;

Measure ordered 10 to 90 percent progress of a declared step

parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
parameter Real referenceStart=0;
parameter Real referenceFinal=1;
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time stepTime=0.5;
输入、输出和物理端口（12 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (12)
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput;
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block RiseTime "Measure ordered 10 to 90 percent progress of a declared step"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real referenceStart=0;
  parameter Real referenceFinal=1;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time stepTime=0.5;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput found(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time t10(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time t90(start=0,fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Real progress;
  Boolean cross10;
  Boolean cross90;
  discrete Boolean found10(start=false,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(referenceFinal>referenceStart,"Step direction and nonzero amplitude are required.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  progress=(u-referenceStart)/(referenceFinal-referenceStart);
  cross10=gate.inWindow and dataOK and time>=stepTime and progress>=0.1;
  cross90=gate.inWindow and dataOK and time>=stepTime and progress>=0.9;
  value=if found then t90-t10 else 0;
  available=found;
algorithm

  when {initial(),cross10,cross90} then
    if cross10 and not found10 then found10:=true; t10:=time; end if;
    if cross90 and found10 and not found then found:=true; t90:=time; end if;
  end when;
end RiseTime;

捕获有限均匀窗口中的离散时标与值

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block SampleHistory "捕获有限均匀窗口中的离散时标与值"
  parameter Integer n(min=2,max=4096) = 128 "Number of ticks to retain";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time startTime = 0 "First tick, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Tick period, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Applicable trajectory";
  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time endTime = startTime+n*samplePeriod "Exclusive end";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Trajectory" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Validity on sampled ticks" ;
  output Real samples[n](each start=0,each fixed=true) "Samples in clock order";
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time stamps[n] = {startTime+(i-1)*samplePeriod for i in 1:n} "Scheduled timestamps";
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Window and data state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput count "Accepted samples" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=startTime,windowEnd=endTime,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,
      minimumSamples=n,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
initial equation
  assert(n>=2 and n<=4096 and samplePeriod>0, "History length or period outside supported range.");
equation
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  count = evidence.validSamples;
  when sample(startTime,samplePeriod) then
    samples = {if evidence.takeTick and dataOK and i==pre(count)+1 then u else pre(samples[i]) for i in 1:n};
  end when;
end SampleHistory;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block SettlingTime "Confirm the last error-band entry at the end of the full window"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;

  parameter Real reference=1;
  parameter Real band(min=0)=0.02;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time stepTime=0.5;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time minDwell(min=0)=0.2;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput settled(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time lastEntry(start=0,fixed=true);
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean inside;
initial equation
  assert(band>=0 and minDwell>=0,"Invalid settling band or dwell.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  inside=gate.inWindow and dataOK and time>=stepTime and abs(u-reference)<=band;
  when {initial(),inside} then lastEntry=if inside then time else 0; end when;
  when gate.complete then settled=pre(inside) and gate.actualStop-pre(lastEntry)>=minDwell and dataStatus==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE; end when;
  value=if settled then lastEntry-stepTime else 0;
  available=settled;
end SettlingTime;

用末段采样估计稳态偏差及残差波动

参数与默认声明（8 项）Parameters and declared defaults (8)
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";
parameter Real biasLimit(min=0) = 0.05 "允许的均值偏差幅值";
parameter Real spreadLimit(min=0) = 0.02 "允许的标准差";
输入、输出和物理端口（9 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (9)
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window";

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block SteadyStateError "用末段采样估计稳态偏差及残差波动"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  parameter Real biasLimit(min=0) = 0.05 "允许的均值偏差幅值";
  parameter Real spreadLimit(min=0) = 0.02 "允许的标准差";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput measured "末段实测值" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference "末段参考值" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput bias "离散有符号均值误差" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput spread "样本总体标准差" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "均值和波动均满足上限才通过";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  EvaluationLibrary.Statistics.SampledMoments moments(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,
    samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples,applicable=applicable);
initial equation
  assert(biasLimit >= 0 and spreadLimit >= 0, "Error limits must be nonnegative.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and abs(measured) < Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(reference) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  moments.valid = dataOK;
  moments.u = if dataOK then measured-reference else 0;
  bias = moments.mean;
  spread = sqrt(moments.variance);
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if abs(bias) <= biasLimit and spread <= spreadLimit then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end SteadyStateError;

within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block TrackingError "计算连续跟踪误差的绝对积分与均方根"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput measured "实测轨迹" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference "目标轨迹" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput error "当前有符号误差" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput absoluteIntegral(start=0,fixed=true) "绝对误差积分，输入单位·s" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput rms "窗口内均方根误差" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Real squareIntegral(start=0,fixed=true);
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and abs(measured) < Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(reference) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  error = if dataOK then measured-reference else 0;
  der(absoluteIntegral) = if inWindow and dataOK then abs(error) else 0;
  der(squareIntegral) = if inWindow and dataOK then error^2 else 0;
  rms = if noEvent(observedDuration > 0) then sqrt(max(0,squareIntegral/observedDuration)) else 0;
end TrackingError;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Response;
block ViolationDuration "Measure cumulative and longest continuous valid violation segments"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput total(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput longest(unit="s") ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput occurrences(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  Boolean during;
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time began(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time completedLongest(start=0,fixed=true);
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;


  during=gate.inWindow and dataOK and u;
  der(total)=if during then 1 else 0;
  when {initial(),change(during)} then
    began=if initial() or during then time else pre(began);
    completedLongest=if initial() then 0 elseif not during then max(pre(completedLongest),time-pre(began)) else pre(completedLongest);
    occurrences=(if initial() then 0 else pre(occurrences))+(if during then 1 else 0);
  end when;
  longest=max(completedLongest,if during then time-began else 0);
  value=total;
  available=gate.validDuration>0;
end ViolationDuration;

partial block PartialWriter "Common finalised result-export contract"
  parameter Integer n(min=1)=1;
  parameter String runId="run" "ASCII letters, digits, underscore or dash";
  parameter String resultSetId="results" "ASCII letters, digits, underscore or dash";
  parameter String outputDirectory="." "Existing directory for exported files";
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WritePolicy writePolicy=
    EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WritePolicy.RejectExisting;
  parameter Boolean logToConsole=true "Print the timestamped outcome";
  parameter Boolean isCSV=false;
  parameter Boolean isConsole=false;
  parameter String extension=".txt";
  parameter String filePath=outputDirectory+"/"+runId+"_"+resultSetId+extension
    "Actual file target; ignored by ConsoleWriter";
  parameter String metricId[n]=fill("metric",n);
  parameter String requirementId[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter String requirementVersion[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter String unitName[n]=fill("1",n);
  parameter String message[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.EvidenceKind evidenceKind[n]=
    fill(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.EvidenceKind.Derived,n);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput u[n] 
  output Boolean ready;
  output Boolean written(start=false,fixed=true);
  output Boolean skippedExisting(start=false,fixed=true);
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.MetricResult records[n];
initial equation
  assert(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.SafeIdentifier(runId) and 
    EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.SafeIdentifier(resultSetId),
    "runId and resultSetId may contain only ASCII letters, digits, '_' or '-'.");
equation
  ready=sum({if u[i].complete then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})==n;
  for i in 1:n loop
    records[i].metricId=metricId[i];
    records[i].requirementId=requirementId[i];
    records[i].requirementVersion=requirementVersion[i];
    records[i].value=u[i].value;
    records[i].valueAvailable=u[i].valueAvailable;
    records[i].unit=unitName[i];
    records[i].dataStatus=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.StatusFromCode(u[i].dataStatus);
    records[i].verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.VerdictFromCode(u[i].verdict);
    records[i].windowStart=windowStart[i];
    records[i].windowStop=windowStop[i];
    records[i].observedDuration=0;
    records[i].validDuration=u[i].validDuration;
    records[i].expectedSamples=u[i].expectedSamples;
    records[i].receivedSamples=u[i].receivedSamples;
    records[i].validSamples=u[i].validSamples;
    records[i].everViolated=u[i].everViolated;
    records[i].firstViolationTime=u[i].firstViolationTime;
    records[i].samplePeriod=samplePeriod[i];
    records[i].evidenceKind=evidenceKind[i];
    records[i].message=message[i];
  end for;
algorithm
  when ready then
    (written,skippedExisting):=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WriteRows(
      filePath,runId,records,writePolicy,isCSV,isConsole,
      resultSetId,time,logToConsole);
  end when;
end PartialWriter;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Results;
block ResultTable "Collect qualified metrics in an inspectable result table"
  parameter Integer n(min=1)=1;
  parameter String runId="run";
  parameter String resultSetId="table";
  parameter Boolean logOnComplete=true;
  parameter String metricId[n]=fill("metric",n);
  parameter String requirementId[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter String requirementVersion[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter String unitName[n]=fill("1",n);
  parameter String message[n]=fill("",n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod[n]=fill(0,n);
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.EvidenceKind evidenceKind[n]=
    fill(EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.EvidenceKind.Derived,n);
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput u[n] 
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput rows[n] 
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.MetricResult records[n];
  output Boolean allComplete;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Integer finalisedCount;
  output Boolean logged(start=false,fixed=true);
equation
  for i in 1:n loop
    connect(u[i],rows[i]);
    records[i].metricId=metricId[i];
    records[i].requirementId=requirementId[i];
    records[i].requirementVersion=requirementVersion[i];
    records[i].value=u[i].value;
    records[i].valueAvailable=u[i].valueAvailable;
    records[i].unit=unitName[i];
    records[i].dataStatus=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.StatusFromCode(u[i].dataStatus);
    records[i].verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.VerdictFromCode(u[i].verdict);
    records[i].windowStart=windowStart[i];
    records[i].windowStop=windowStop[i];
    records[i].observedDuration=0;
    records[i].validDuration=u[i].validDuration;
    records[i].expectedSamples=u[i].expectedSamples;
    records[i].receivedSamples=u[i].receivedSamples;
    records[i].validSamples=u[i].validSamples;
    records[i].everViolated=u[i].everViolated;
    records[i].firstViolationTime=u[i].firstViolationTime;
    records[i].samplePeriod=samplePeriod[i];
    records[i].evidenceKind=evidenceKind[i];
    records[i].message=message[i];
  end for;
  finalisedCount=sum({if u[i].complete then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n});
  allComplete=finalisedCount==n;
  complete=allComplete;
algorithm
  when allComplete then
    if logOnComplete then
      logged:=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.LogCompletion(
        "Result table finalised","in-memory table","Modelica variable: records",
        runId,resultSetId,n,time,"SUCCESS");
    else
      logged:=false;
    end if;
  end when;
end ResultTable;

Aggregate required verdicts, preserving witnesses

  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
output Boolean complete;
output Boolean everViolated;
output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;

output Boolean logged(start=false,fixed=true);

直接引用此类的示例：Examples directly referencing this class: ResultAggregationAndFiles

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Results;
block VerdictAggregator "Aggregate required verdicts, preserving witnesses"
  parameter Integer n(min=1)=1;
  parameter Boolean required[n]=fill(true,n);
  parameter String runId="run";
  parameter String resultSetId="aggregate";
  parameter Boolean logOnComplete=true;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricInput u[n] 
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  output Integer nApplicable;
  output Boolean logged(start=false,fixed=true);
equation
  nApplicable=sum({if required[i] and u[i].verdict<>5 then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n});
  complete=sum({if not required[i] or u[i].complete then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})==n;
  everViolated=sum({if required[i] and u[i].everViolated then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0;
  firstViolationTime=if everViolated then min(
    {if required[i] and u[i].everViolated then u[i].firstViolationTime 
      else Modelica.Constants.inf for i in 1:n}) else 0;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.Aggregate(
    {u[i].verdict for i in 1:n},required);
  dataStatus=if nApplicable==0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE 
    else if sum({if required[i] and u[i].dataStatus==4 then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID 
    else if sum({if required[i] and u[i].dataStatus==3 then 1 else 0 for i in 1:n})>0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE 
    else if not complete then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING 
    else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE;
algorithm
  when complete then
    if logOnComplete then
      logged:=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.LogCompletion(
        "Aggregate verdict finalised: "+
          EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.VerdictText(verdict),
        "aggregate verdict","Modelica variable: verdict",
        runId,resultSetId,nApplicable,time,"SUCCESS");
    else
      logged:=false;
    end if;
  end when;
end VerdictAggregator;

within EvaluationLibrary.Signals;
block AbsoluteError "Compute absolute paired error"
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput y ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
equation

Compute a finite difference only after two qualified samples

参数与默认声明（3 项）Parameters and declared defaults (3)
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u;
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput;

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. 实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Signals;
block FiniteDifference "Compute a finite difference only after two qualified samples"
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput y(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available(start=false,fixed=true) ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trust ;
  discrete Real previous(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean wasValid(start=false,fixed=true);
  Boolean eligible;
initial equation
  assert(samplePeriod>0,"Positive sample period required.");
equation
  connect(valid,trust);
  if not useValidInput then trust=true; end if;
  eligible=trust and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  when sample(sampleStart,samplePeriod) then
    available=eligible and pre(wasValid);
    y=if available then (u-pre(previous))/samplePeriod else 0;
    previous=u;
    wasValid=eligible;
  end when;
end FiniteDifference;

Compute relative error with an explicit denominator floor

within EvaluationLibrary.Signals;
block RelativeError "Compute relative error with an explicit denominator floor"
parameter Real referenceFloor(min=0)=1e-9;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput y ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
initial equation
  assert(referenceFloor>0,"Reference floor must be positive.");
equation
  available=abs(reference)>=referenceFloor and abs(reference)<Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  y=if available then (u-reference)/abs(reference) else 0;
end RelativeError;

block WrappedAngleError "Compute wrapped angular direction error"
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput angle(unit="rad") ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput reference(unit="rad") ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput y(unit="rad") ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
equation
  available=abs(angle)<Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(reference)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  y=if available then Modelica.Math.atan2(sin(angle-reference),cos(angle-reference)) else 0;
end WrappedAngleError;

within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block ControlEffort "对执行量计算归一化作用量和满载时间"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Real ratedMagnitude(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1 "额定执行量幅值";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput applied "实测执行量" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput squaredEffort(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "归一化执行量的平方时间积分" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput ratedDuration(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "幅值大于或等于额定值的时间" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Real normalized;
initial equation
  assert(ratedMagnitude > 0, "Rated magnitude must be positive.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and abs(applied) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  normalized = if dataOK then applied/ratedMagnitude else 0;
  der(squaredEffort) = if inWindow and dataOK then normalized^2 else 0;
  der(ratedDuration) = if inWindow and dataOK and abs(normalized) >= 1 then 1 else 0;
end ControlEffort;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block EnergyEfficiency "按已定义输入和有用输出功率计算能量效率"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Energy minimumInput(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 1e-3 "最小有效输入能量，J";
  parameter Real requiredEfficiency(min=0,max=1) = 0.8 "通过的最低效率";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput inputPower(unit="W") "非负输入功率" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput usefulPower(unit="W") "非负有用输出功率" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput inputEnergy(unit="J",start=0,fixed=true) "输入能量" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput usefulEnergy(unit="J",start=0,fixed=true) "有用输出能量" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput efficiency "输出能量除以输入能量" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "效率判定";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
initial equation
  assert(minimumInput > 0 and requiredEfficiency >= 0 and requiredEfficiency <= 1, "Invalid efficiency limits.");
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and abs(inputPower) < Modelica.Constants.inf and abs(usefulPower) < Modelica.Constants.inf and inputPower >= 0 and usefulPower >= 0;
  der(inputEnergy) = if inWindow and dataOK then inputPower else 0;
  der(usefulEnergy) = if inWindow and dataOK then usefulPower else 0;
  efficiency = if inputEnergy >= minimumInput then usefulEnergy/inputEnergy else 0;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if inputEnergy < minimumInput then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED elseif efficiency >= requiredEfficiency then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end EnergyEfficiency;
