parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block GatedPeak "只对危险标记内的采样计算严重度峰值"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  parameter Real peakLimit = 1 "可接受的危险严重度峰值上界";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput hazard "独立的危险标记" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput severity "严重度轨迹" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput hazardSamples(start=0,fixed=true) "有效危险样本数" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput peak(start=0,fixed=true) "危险样本最大值" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput peakTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "峰值最早时刻" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict "采样危险峰值判定";
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and (not hazard or abs(severity) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    hazardSamples = pre(hazardSamples)+(if takeTick and dataOK and hazard then 1 else 0);
    if takeTick and dataOK and hazard and (pre(hazardSamples) == 0 or severity > pre(peak)) then
      peak = severity;
      peakTime = time;
    else
      peak = pre(peak);
      peakTime = pre(peakTime);
    end if;
  end when;
  verdict = if dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif dataState == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED
    else if hazardSamples == 0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE elseif peak <= peakLimit then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED;
end GatedPeak;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block Integral "Integral over the qualified continuous interval"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  Modelica.Units.SI.Time duration;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousAccumulator acc;
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.integrate=gate.inWindow and dataOK;
  duration=gate.validDuration;
  available=duration>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then acc.total else 0;
end Integral;

均匀窗的最小二乘线性趋势

参数与默认声明（7 项）Parameters and declared defaults (7)

parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";
parameter Real referenceOffset = 0 "Numerical centering in the input unit";
输入、输出和物理端口（8 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (8)
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window";
output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Observed signal";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput slope "Least-squares slope, input unit per second";

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block LinearTrend "均匀窗的最小二乘线性趋势"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  parameter Real referenceOffset = 0 "Numerical centering in the input unit";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Observed signal" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput slope "Least-squares slope, input unit per second" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput intercept "Fit at the first clock instant" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput defined "At least two time-distinct valid samples" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  discrete Real sumT(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real sumTT(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real sumX(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Real sumTX(start=0,fixed=true);
  Real tRelative(unit="s");
  Real xCentered;
  Real denominator;
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  tRelative = time-firstSample;
  xCentered = if dataOK then u-referenceOffset else 0;
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    sumT = pre(sumT)+(if takeTick and dataOK then tRelative else 0);
    sumTT = pre(sumTT)+(if takeTick and dataOK then tRelative^2 else 0);
    sumX = pre(sumX)+(if takeTick and dataOK then xCentered else 0);
    sumTX = pre(sumTX)+(if takeTick and dataOK then tRelative*xCentered else 0);
  end when;
  denominator = validSamples*sumTT-sumT^2;
  defined = validSamples >= 2 and noEvent(denominator > 0) and dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID;
  slope = if defined then (validSamples*sumTX-sumT*sumX)/denominator else 0;
  intercept = if defined then referenceOffset+(sumX-slope*sumT)/validSamples else 0;
end LinearTrend;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput argmaxTime(unit="s") ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput peakToPeak ;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampleAccumulator acc;
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;

  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);

  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.take=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  nValid=acc.count;
  available=acc.count>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then maximum else 0;
  minimum=acc.minimum;
  maximum=acc.maximum;
  argminTime=acc.argminTime;
  argmaxTime=acc.argmaxTime;
  peakToPeak=maximum-minimum;
end SampleExtrema;

within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block SampleRMS "RMS on the explicit qualified sampling grid"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  output Integer nValid;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampleAccumulator acc;
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.take=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  nValid=acc.count;
  available=acc.count>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then sqrt(max(0,acc.sumSquare/acc.count)) else 0;
end SampleRMS;

block SampleVariance "Welford sample variance with a declared degrees correction"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  output Integer nValid;
  parameter Integer ddof(min=0)=1;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=true,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampleAccumulator acc;
initial equation
  assert(ddof>=0,"Negative degrees correction.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=gate.complete and acc.count>0 and acc.count<=ddof;
  violationNow=(if true then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.take=gate.tick and gate.sampleInWindow and dataOK;
  nValid=acc.count;
  available=acc.count>ddof and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then max(0,acc.m2)/(acc.count-ddof) else 0;
end SampleVariance;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block SampledMoments "以稳定的递推计算离散均值和方差"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "均匀时钟上的待统计轨迹" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput mean(start=0,fixed=true) "样本均值" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput variance "总体形式方差 M2/N" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput rms "样本均方根" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  discrete Real m2(start=0,fixed=true);
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    if takeTick and dataOK then
      mean = pre(mean)+(u-pre(mean))/max(1,validSamples);
      m2 = pre(m2)+(u-pre(mean))*(u-mean);
    else
      mean = pre(mean);
      m2 = pre(m2);
    end if;
  end when;
  variance = if validSamples > 0 then max(0,m2/validSamples) else 0;
  rms = sqrt(mean^2+variance);
end SampledMoments;

  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  Modelica.Units.SI.Time duration;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousAccumulator acc;
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;

  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=false;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);

    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;

      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);

  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;

  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;

  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.integrate=gate.inWindow and dataOK;
  duration=gate.validDuration;
  available=duration>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then acc.total/gate.validDuration else 0;

  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.integrate=gate.inWindow and dataOK;
  duration=gate.validDuration;
  available=duration>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then sqrt(max(0,acc.squareTotal/gate.validDuration)) else 0;
end TimeRMS;

within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block TimeVariance "Duration-weighted second central moment"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid if useValidInput ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput window if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataStatus;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean everViolated;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstViolationTime;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Interfaces.MetricOutput result ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput available ;
  parameter Real roundoffTolerance(min=0)=1e-10;
  Real rawVariance;
  Modelica.Units.SI.Time duration;
protected
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput trusted ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput externalWindow ;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.WindowEvidence gate(windowMode=windowMode,windowStart=windowStart,windowStop=windowStop,
    sampled=false,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleStart=sampleStart,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK;
  Boolean violationNow;
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousAccumulator acc;
initial equation
  assert(roundoffTolerance>=0,"Negative variance roundoff tolerance.");
equation
  connect(valid,trusted);
  connect(window,externalWindow);
  if not useValidInput then trusted=true; end if;
  if windowMode<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle then externalWindow=false; end if;
  dataOK=trusted and abs(u)<Modelica.Constants.inf;
  gate.rawWindow=externalWindow;
  gate.dataOK=dataOK;
  gate.overflow=rawVariance < -roundoffTolerance;
  violationNow=(if false then gate.sampleInWindow else gate.inWindow) and dataOK and (false);
  when {initial(),violationNow} then
    everViolated=(not initial() and pre(everViolated)) or violationNow;
    firstViolationTime=if violationNow and (initial() or not pre(everViolated)) then time
      else if initial() then 0 else pre(firstViolationTime);
  end when;
  dataStatus=gate.status;
  verdict=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.ToVerdict(dataStatus,everViolated,false);
  complete=gate.complete;
  result.value=value;
  result.valueAvailable=available and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  result.dataStatus=Integer(dataStatus);
  result.verdict=Integer(verdict);
  result.everViolated=everViolated;
  result.firstViolationTime=firstViolationTime;
  result.validDuration=gate.validDuration;
  result.expectedSamples=gate.expectedSamples;
  result.receivedSamples=gate.receivedSamples;
  result.validSamples=gate.validSamples;
  result.complete=complete;

  connect(u,acc.u);
  acc.integrate=gate.inWindow and dataOK;
  duration=gate.validDuration;
  available=duration>0 and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID and dataStatus<>EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE;
  value=if available then max(0,rawVariance) else 0;
  rawVariance=if duration>0 then acc.squareTotal/duration-(acc.total/duration)^2 else 0;
end TimeVariance;

block TotalVariation "求相邻有效样本的绝对差累计"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples "Accepted observations" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "待评价输入" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput value(start=0,fixed=true) "离散总变差" ;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.SampledEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable,samplePeriod=samplePeriod,sampleOffset=sampleOffset,minimumSamples=minimumSamples);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  Boolean takeTick "Only the scheduled half-open grid is accepted";
  discrete Real previousValue(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean previousValid(start=false,fixed=true);
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  validSamples = evidence.validSamples;
  takeTick = evidence.takeTick;
  dataOK = valid and abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf;
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    previousValid = takeTick and dataOK;
    previousValue = if previousValid then u else pre(previousValue);
    value = if previousValid and pre(previousValid) then pre(value)+abs(u-pre(previousValue)) else pre(value);
  end when;
end TotalVariation;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Statistics;
block ZeroCrossingOscillation "由相同方向穿越估计振荡周期"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Host-provided input validity" ;

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s") "Accumulated valid duration" ;
  parameter Real level = 0 "Reference level in the signal unit";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Observed waveform" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput crossings(start=0,fixed=true) "Observed upward crossings" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput meanPeriod(unit="s") "First-to-last span divided by complete cycles" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput defined "At least one complete observed cycle" ;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstCrossing(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time lastCrossing(start=0,fixed=true);
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.ContinuousEvidence evidence(windowStart=windowStart,windowEnd=windowEnd,applicable=applicable);
  Boolean dataOK "Validity combined with the metric data domain";
  Boolean high;
equation
  evidence.dataOK = dataOK;
  dataState = evidence.dataState;
  inWindow = evidence.inWindow;
  observedDuration = evidence.observedDuration;
  dataOK = valid and noEvent(abs(u) < Modelica.Constants.inf);
  high = u > level;
  when {initial(),high} then
    if not initial() and inWindow and dataOK then
      crossings = pre(crossings)+1;
      firstCrossing = if pre(crossings)==0 then time else pre(firstCrossing);
      lastCrossing = time;
    else
      crossings = pre(crossings);
      firstCrossing = pre(firstCrossing);
      lastCrossing = pre(lastCrossing);
    end if;
  end when;
  defined = crossings >= 2 and dataState <> EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID;
  meanPeriod = if defined then (lastCrossing-firstCrossing)/(crossings-1) else 0;
end ZeroCrossingOscillation;

EXAMPLE 01

AnalyticStatistics
斜坡的连续与采样统计Continuous and sampled statistics of a ramp

研究问题Question

为什么同一信号的时间均值与采样均值不完全相同？Why do time and sample means differ for the same signal?

如何实现How it is assembled

inputSignal.y=time，窗口 [0,2)，内部采样周期 0.01 s；连续积分和采样统计并行连接。inputSignal.y=time is observed over [0,2), with 0.01 s internal sampling. Continuous and sampled statistics are connected in parallel.

应当观察到什么What to look for

连续积分为 2，时间均值 1，时间 RMS=2/√3，时间方差 1/3。采样点是 0 到 1.99 的 200 点，采样均值为 0.995；差异来自证据定义，不是求解器必然出错。The integral is 2, time mean 1, time RMS 2/√3 and time variance 1/3. There are 200 sample points from 0 to 1.99, yielding sample mean 0.995. The difference follows the evidence definition, not necessarily a solver error.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把内部 samplePeriod 改为 0.1，再手算采样均值 0.95；另行修改输出网格，确认它不是同一个设置。Set internal samplePeriod to 0.1 and calculate the expected sample mean 0.95. Change the output grid separately to distinguish the settings.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

最终解释前先确认窗口已结束且数据有效；运行中输出是尚在积累的量。Confirm finalisation and qualification before interpreting the final result; values during execution are still accumulating.

重点观察变量Variables to inspect

integralValuetimeMeanValuetimeRMSValuetimeVarianceValuesampleMeanValuesampleMean.nValidsampleRangeValue

EXAMPLE 02

BoundsAndValidWitness
保留违规证据而不掩盖后续无效数据Retain a witness without hiding later invalidity

研究问题Question

先发生有效违规，之后数据失效，最终应怎样报告？How should a valid violation followed by invalid data be reported?

如何实现How it is assembled

两个上界为 1.2 的评价器观察 t，其中一个在 [1.6,1.7) s 失去资格；第三个上界为 3。共同窗口结束于 2 s。Two upper bounds of 1.2 observe t, with one becoming invalid over [1.6,1.7) s. A third upper bound is 3. All windows end at 2 s.

应当观察到什么What to look for

一直有效的支路为 VIOLATED；有失效段的支路为 INVALID，但仍保留在约 1.2 s 越界的 everViolated 与 firstViolationTime；第三支路为 SATISFIED。The always-valid branch is VIOLATED. The gapped branch is INVALID while retaining the witness near 1.2 s and firstViolationTime. The third branch is SATISFIED.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把无效段提前到越界前，比较是否仍有合格违规证据；分别报告数据状态和判定。Move the invalid interval before the crossing and compare qualified witnesses, reporting status and verdict separately.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

INVALID 不能抹去真实已有的违规，也不能因为存在违规就把所有后续数据称为有效。INVALID does not erase an earlier valid witness, and a witness does not qualify all subsequent data.

重点观察变量Variables to inspect

alwaysValid.dataStatusalwaysValid.verdictwithGap.dataStatuswithGap.verdictwithGap.everViolatedwithGap.firstViolationTimepassing.verdict

源码声明的实验设置：Declared experiment settings: StartTime=0 · StopTime=2.1 · Interval=0.004200000000000001 · Tolerance=1e-6

装配组件及明确设置的参数Instantiated components and explicit parameter settings
Modelica.Blocks.Sources.RealExpression inputSignal(y=time);
Modelica.Blocks.Sources.BooleanExpression qualified(y=time<1.6 or time>=1.7);
EvaluationLibrary.Requirements.UpperBound alwaysValid(upper=1.2,windowStop=2);
EvaluationLibrary.Requirements.UpperBound withGap(upper=1.2,useValidInput=true,windowStop=2);
EvaluationLibrary.Requirements.UpperBound passing(upper=3,windowStop=2);
实际 connect 连线Actual connect statements

connect(alwaysValid.value,alwaysValidValue);
connect(inputSignal.y,withGap.u);
connect(withGap.value,withGapValue);

connect(passing.value,passingValue);
connect(qualified.y,withGap.valid);

EXAMPLE 03

ConsistencyAndLabels
分类准确率与数值一致性Classification accuracy and numerical consistency

研究问题Question

为什么通道彼此一致不能证明分类判断正确？Why does channel consistency not establish classification correctness?

如何实现How it is assembled

四个标签采样时刻为 0.5、1.5、2.5、3.5 s；truth 在 2 s 变真，decision 在 [1,3) 为真。另有三个恒定数值通道 1、2、3。Labels are sampled at 0.5, 1.5, 2.5 and 3.5 s. truth becomes true at 2 s and decision is true over [1,3). Separate numerical channels are fixed at 1, 2 and 3.

应当观察到什么What to look for

四次分类依次产生 TN、FP、TP、FN，各计数为 1，accuracy=0.5。数值通道 spread=2，与上述分类对错不是同一个指标。The samples produce TN, FP, TP and FN, each once, giving accuracy=0.5. The numerical spread is 2, which measures a different property.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

改变 decision 的时间窗，手算混淆矩阵；再单独改变第三个数值通道。Change the decision window and calculate the confusion matrix, then vary the third numerical channel separately.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

样本数量与采样相位会影响统计；没有类别样本时相关比例的分母可能不可用。Sample count and sampling phase affect statistics; ratios may be unavailable when a class has no observations.

EXAMPLE 04

DecayMeasurement
正峰值衰减比Positive-peak decay ratio

研究问题Question

怎样把衰减振荡转成峰值比，而不把每个样本都当成峰？How is decay measured from peaks rather than every sample?

如何实现How it is assembled

输入 exp(-t) sin(2π·5t)，采样周期 0.001 s，窗口终止于 3 s；模块识别合格的正峰值。The input is exp(-t) sin(2π·5t), sampled every 0.001 s until 3 s. The component identifies qualified positive peaks.

应当观察到什么What to look for

相邻同类峰相隔约 0.2 s，因此解析幅值比约 exp(-0.2)=0.81873，对数减量约 0.2；离散采样识别会引入小偏差。Successive like-signed peaks are about 0.2 s apart, so the analytical ratio is exp(-0.2)=0.81873 and logarithmic decrement about 0.2. Discrete peak identification introduces small differences.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

逐步加大 samplePeriod，观察漏峰或峰值估计误差。Increase samplePeriod progressively and inspect missed peaks or amplitude error.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

只有足够合格峰值才有比值意义；噪声、多频和变频需要额外分析。The ratio requires sufficient qualified peaks; noise, multiple tones and changing frequency need additional interpretation.

EXAMPLE 05

FirstOrderResponse
上升时间与终点稳定时间Rise time and terminal settling time

研究问题Question

响应第一次入带为何不一定就是最终稳定时间？Why is the first band entry not necessarily final settling?

如何实现How it is assembled

0.5 s 的单位阶跃驱动 T=0.5 一阶本体，评价窗口为 [0.5,4)。上升时间使用声明的阈值，稳定时间使用目标 1、误差带 0.02 和最短驻留。A unit step at 0.5 s drives a first-order host with T=0.5 over [0.5,4). Rise time uses declared levels; settling uses target 1, band 0.02 and a minimum dwell.

应当观察到什么What to look for

默认 10%–90% 上升时间解析值为 0.5 ln(9)≈1.0986 s；2% 带的入带时延为 0.5 ln(50)≈1.9560 s。最终稳定资格须在完整窗口结束时确认，而不是首次入带就立即证明。The default 10–90% rise time is 0.5 ln(9)≈1.0986 s. Entry into the 2% band occurs after 0.5 ln(50)≈1.9560 s. Final settling qualification requires the complete window end, not merely first entry.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

缩短 windowStop，使末段驻留不足；观察数值可用性与 settled 如何变化。Shorten windowStop so that terminal dwell is insufficient and inspect availability and settled.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

这里的解析比较依赖单调一阶响应；振荡响应可能反复进出误差带。The analytical comparison uses a monotonic first-order response; an oscillatory response can enter and leave repeatedly.

重点观察变量Variables to inspect

EXAMPLE 06

HarmonicMeasurements
相干采样窗口中的两个频率Two coherent tones in a finite sample window

研究问题Question

完整采样窗如何支撑谱幅值和谐波畸变？How does a complete sample window support amplitudes and harmonic distortion?

如何实现How it is assembled

输入为 5 Hz、幅值 1 的正弦加上 10 Hz、幅值 0.1 的正弦；nSamples=200、samplePeriod=0.01、windowStop=2。The input combines a 5 Hz sine of amplitude 1 and a 10 Hz sine of amplitude 0.1, using 200 samples at 0.01 s over a 2 s window.

应当观察到什么What to look for

采样频率 100 Hz、频率间隔 0.5 Hz；两个频率都与网格相干。基频幅值应约 1，二次谐波约 0.1；THD 比例约 0.1，即 10%。同时检查缓冲完成与数据资格。Sampling frequency is 100 Hz and frequency spacing 0.5 Hz; both tones are coherent with the grid. Fundamental amplitude should be about 1, second harmonic about 0.1 and THD about 0.1, or 10%. Inspect acquisition completion and qualification.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把频率改为不落在网格上的值，观察有限窗泄漏；再改变采样周期，检查 Nyquist 边界。Move a tone off the grid and inspect finite-window leakage, then change the sampling period and check the Nyquist limit.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

相干纯正弦案例不保证任意非平稳信号都能得到相同精度的幅相解释。A coherent sinusoidal example does not guarantee equally accurate amplitude/phase interpretation for arbitrary non-stationary signals.

EXAMPLE 07

IncompleteWindow
正常结束但观测未完整Normal termination with incomplete observation

研究问题Question

正常终止仿真能否直接得到满足结论？Does normal simulation termination imply satisfaction?

如何实现How it is assembled

两个评价器都要求观察到 2 s，但该示例在 1 s 正常停止；上界分别为 3 与 0.5。Both evaluators request observation until 2 s, but the example normally terminates at 1 s. Upper bounds are 3 and 0.5.

应当观察到什么What to look for

两者最终 complete 可以为真而 dataStatus=INCOMPLETE。无违规证据的支路保持 UNDECIDED；另一个保留约 0.5 s 的违规并判为 VIOLATED。Both can finalise with complete=true and dataStatus=INCOMPLETE. The branch without a witness remains UNDECIDED; the other retains the violation near 0.5 s and is VIOLATED.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把 StopTime 延长到 2.1，比较完整覆盖后无违规支路的判定。Extend StopTime to 2.1 and inspect the no-violation branch after complete coverage.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

此处研究正常 terminal 事件，不声称异常崩溃或强杀进程也能完成同样的收尾。This examines normal terminal handling, not a guarantee of identical finalisation after crashes or forced process termination.

EXAMPLE 08

PairedPerformance
完整可比积分的改进量Improvement between complete comparable integrals

研究问题Question

怎样明确“变小是好还是坏”，并避免零分母误读？How can preferred direction and denominator validity be made explicit?

如何实现How it is assembled

基准为 t，候选为 0.8t，同在 [0,2) 积分；Improvement 使用声明的配对标识、单位和窗口，以及默认 LowerIsBetter。Baseline is t and candidate 0.8t, both integrated over [0,2). Improvement uses declared comparison metadata and default LowerIsBetter.

应当观察到什么What to look for

基准积分 2、候选 1.6；absoluteChange=0.4，relativeChange=0.2，即 20% 而不是数值 20。相对值还须 relativeAvailable=true。Baseline integral is 2 and candidate 1.6. absoluteChange=0.4 and relativeChange=0.2, meaning 20%, not the number 20. Relative interpretation also requires relativeAvailable=true.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把 sense 改为 HigherIsBetter，观察符号变化；另做接近零基准的实验。Change sense to HigherIsBetter and inspect the sign, then separately test a near-zero baseline.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

数学上可相减不等于工程上可比较；元数据和指标定义需要研究者保持一致。Mathematical subtraction does not establish engineering comparability; the researcher must align definitions and metadata.

EXAMPLE 09

RepeatedRequestResponse
匹配、超时与删失分开计数Separate matching, timeout and censoring

研究问题Question

观测结束前发出的最后一个请求是否应该算超时？Should the last request before observation end count as a timeout?

如何实现How it is assembled

请求在 1、2、3、3.95 s；响应在 0.5、1.1、2.15 s；deadline=0.2 s，观测截止 4 s。Requests occur at 1, 2, 3 and 3.95 s; responses at 0.5, 1.1 and 2.15 s. Deadline is 0.2 s and observation ends at 4 s.

应当观察到什么What to look for

1 与 2 s 的请求分别以 0.1 和 0.15 s 匹配；3 s 请求超时；3.95 s 请求在到达截止期限前观测结束，因此删失。0.5 s 响应没有对应请求。预期 matched=2、timedOut=1、censored=1、unmatchedResponse=1。The first two requests match after 0.1 and 0.15 s. The 3 s request times out. Observation ends before the 3.95 s request deadline, so it is censored. The 0.5 s response is unmatched: expected counts are 2 matches, 1 timeout, 1 censored request and 1 unmatched response.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

把窗口延长到 4.3 s，比较最后请求从删失转为超时的条件。Extend the window to 4.3 s and inspect when the final request becomes a timeout rather than censored.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

没有响应和已超时不是同一事实；队列容量不足还可能使证据无效。No response and an elapsed deadline are different facts; queue overflow can also invalidate evidence.

重点观察变量Variables to inspect

paired.matchedpaired.timedOutpaired.censoredpaired.unmatchedResponserequirement.verdict

Modelica.Blocks.Sources.BooleanExpression responses(y=(time>=0.5 and time<0.525) or (time>=1.1 and time<1.125) or (time>=2.15 and time<2.175));

EXAMPLE 10

ResultAggregationAndFiles
三种结果的聚合与文件输出Aggregate three verdicts and export results

研究问题Question

怎样把通过、违规、无效三条结果保留下来并知道文件生成在哪里？How can satisfied, violated and invalid results be preserved with a known export location?

如何实现How it is assembled

三条 UpperBound 在 2 s 完成：passing 上界 3，violating 上界 1，invalid 上界 3 但中间失去资格；table 分发到 aggregate 和 CSV、TXT、console 三种写出器。Three UpperBound instances finalise at 2 s: passing has upper 3, violating upper 1, and invalid upper 3 with a qualification gap. table distributes them to aggregate and three writers.

应当观察到什么What to look for

预期三个记录依次表达满足、违规与无效；综合判定 INVALID，但保留子项违规证据。文件名为 demo_csv.csv 和 demo_text.txt，控制台还打印结果及带绝对路径的完成日志。重复运行保留文件时应打印 SKIPPED。The three records should express satisfaction, violation and invalidity. Aggregate verdict is INVALID while retaining a child witness. Files are demo_csv.csv and demo_text.txt; terminal output includes results and completion logs with absolute paths. A repeat run retaining files should report SKIPPED.

自己动手修改Controlled variation

先在新目录运行，再原目录重复运行；随后明确选择 Overwrite，检查日志动作与文件内容。同步修改 table 和 writer 的标识元数据。Run in a fresh directory, repeat without deleting files, then explicitly select Overwrite and inspect both logs and content. Update table and writer metadata together.

能够得出的结论与边界Interpretation and limits

v0.3.1 的结果组件已在源码层恢复，但本次没有重新编译仿真；必须在目标工具运行后再将输出视为实际验证。成功写文件也不意味着综合判定 SATISFIED。The v0.3.1 reporting components are restored at source level but were not compiled or simulated in this documentation work. Verify them in the target tool. Successful file writing does not imply SATISFIED.

07

从案例迁移到自己的科研实验Move from examples to your own research experiment

先把研究要求写成可检查的量、窗口、阈值和有效性规则，再选对应评价器。保留指标值、状态、判定、首次违规和覆盖信息。对于对照实验，尽量让基准与候选共用同一窗口和输入定义。Express the research requirement as quantity, window, criterion and validity rule before choosing an evaluator. Preserve value, status, verdict, first violation and coverage. Use matching windows and inputs for baseline–candidate comparisons.

下方模型从零连接一个上界需求和 CSV 写出器。输出目录默认是仿真进程工作目录，不保证等于源码目录；以控制台 destination 日志为准。不要把重复运行的 SKIPPED 当成新结果生成。The model below connects an upper-bound requirement to a CSV writer. The default directory is the simulation process working directory, not necessarily the source directory; use the logged destination. SKIPPED on a repeated run is not a newly generated result.

within;
model MyBoundReport
  Modelica.Blocks.Sources.RealExpression signal(y=time);
  EvaluationLibrary.Requirements.UpperBound limit(upper=1, windowStop=2);
  EvaluationLibrary.Results.CSVWriter report(
    n=1, metricId={"upper-limit"}, requirementVersion={"1.0"},
    windowStart={0}, windowStop={2}, runId="study", resultSetId="checks");
equation
  connect(signal.y, limit.u);
  connect(limit.result, report.u[1]);
  annotation(experiment(StopTime=2.1, Tolerance=1e-6));
end MyBoundReport;
上述片段是教学实验模板，不替代目标工具的翻译检查。保存为单独文件并在已加载本库后打开；不要直接覆盖库内的正式模型。The snippet is a teaching experiment template, not a substitute for translation checks in the intended tool. Save it separately and load it after this library; do not overwrite distributed models.
必须记录Record记录方法How to record it
源码与环境Source and environment本库提交号、本体提交号、MSL 版本、工具版本和运行平台。Library and host commits, MSL version, tool version and execution platform.
干预与场景Intervention and scenario参数值、初始状态、接线拓扑、输入时序、各个可选端口是否启用。Parameter values, initial states, topology, input timing and every enabled optional connector.
数值设置Numerical settings仿真起止时刻、求解器、容差、最大步长（如设置）、结果输出网格、内部采样网格。Start/stop times, solver, tolerance, maximum step where set, output grid and internal sampling grid.
结论与证据Conclusion and evidence实际曲线、关键事件时刻、无效与不完整状态、与预期不符的内容。Actual traces, event times, invalid/incomplete states and discrepancies from the expected behaviour.
08

排错、适用边界与常见误读Troubleshooting, scope and common misinterpretations

结果数值为零但 available=falseThe result is zero but unavailable

零可能是占位值。读取 valueAvailable 或 available，再结合 dataStatus；同样，firstViolationTime=0 且 everViolated=false 不代表 t=0 已违规。Zero can be a placeholder. Inspect availability and dataStatus. Similarly, firstViolationTime=0 with everViolated=false does not mean a violation occurred at t=0.

complete=true 为什么不满足Why does complete=true not imply satisfaction?

complete 是收尾状态。提前终止、无效输入或没有需求判据都可能使最终结果不是 SATISFIED；检查 dataStatus 与 verdict 的独立字段。complete indicates finalisation. Early termination, invalid inputs or the absence of a requirement criterion can prevent SATISFIED; inspect status and verdict separately.

没有生成新文件Why was no new file generated?

检查所有 u[i].complete 是否达到 true、输出目录是否已存在、文件策略是否跳过已有文件、以及实际仿真工作目录。ConsoleWriter 本来就不创建文件。Check all input completion flags, directory existence, existing-file policy and the actual simulation working directory. ConsoleWriter intentionally creates no file.

文件中的单位或窗口与指标不同Exported units or windows differ from the metric

这些元数据来自写出器参数，不自动从数值连接器推断。应由同一实验配置显式设置，并在导出前审查，而不是让文件名替代需求追溯。Writer parameters supply this metadata; it is not inferred from the numeric connector. Set it from a common experiment configuration and review it before export; a filename does not provide requirement traceability.

可以用一次仿真证明系统安全吗Can one simulation prove system safety?

本库判定只对已建模本体、已执行场景、已声明窗口和资格规则成立。模型保真度、未覆盖场景和工程认证是另一个层面的证据。Verdicts apply to the model, executed scenario, declared window and qualification rules. Model fidelity, unexamined scenarios and engineering certification require separate evidence.

报错时先定位最小问题：加载失败、模型不平衡、初始化冲突、积分失败、输出不符合预期，是不同层次的问题。提交 issue 时给出最小模型、参数、工具版本、完整诊断和预期结果。不要用放宽容差掩盖接线或物理定义错误。Separate loading failures, structural imbalance, initialisation conflicts, integration failures and unexpected outputs. An issue should include a minimal model, parameters, tool version, complete diagnostics and expected behaviour. Do not use looser tolerances to conceal topology or physical-definition errors.

这些定义用于类型、连接器、数值函数、内部实现或示例支撑。它们也全部收录，但不应把 Internal 或 Views 中的演示辅助类当作稳定的公共应用接口。These definitions support types, connectors, numerical functions, internal implementation or demonstrations. They are included for completeness; Internal and Views helpers should not be assumed to be stable public application interfaces.

Combine required verdict codes using an explicit priority

input Integer codes[:];
input Boolean required[size(codes,1)];
output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. Check its interfaces, translation diagnostics and results in a minimal model. An example in the same group is not complete validation of this class.

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions;
function Aggregate "Combine required verdict codes using an explicit priority"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input Integer codes[:];
  input Boolean required[size(codes,1)];
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
protected
  Integer applicableCount;
  Boolean invalid;
  Boolean violated;
  Boolean undecided;
algorithm
  applicableCount:=0; invalid:=false; violated:=false; undecided:=false;
  for i in 1:size(codes,1) loop
    if required[i] and codes[i]<>5 then
      applicableCount:=applicableCount+1;
      invalid:=invalid or EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.VerdictFromCode(codes[i])==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID;
      violated:=violated or codes[i]==3;
      undecided:=undecided or codes[i]==1;
    end if;
  end for;
  verdict:=if applicableCount==0 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE elseif invalid then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID
    elseif violated then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED elseif undecided then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED;
end Aggregate;

Render a finalized row without fabricating an unavailable value

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions;
function FormatRow "Render a finalized row without fabricating an unavailable value"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input String runId;
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.MetricResult row;
  input Boolean csv=false;
  output String text;
protected
  String numeric;
  String witness;
  String status;
  String verdict;
algorithm
  numeric:=if row.valueAvailable then String(row.value,significantDigits=14) else "";
  witness:=if row.everViolated then String(row.firstViolationTime,significantDigits=14) else "";
  status:=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.StatusText(row.dataStatus);
  verdict:=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.VerdictText(row.verdict);
  text:=if csv then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.QuoteCSV(runId)+","+EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.QuoteCSV(row.metricId)+","+EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.QuoteCSV(row.requirementVersion)+
    ","+numeric+","+String(row.valueAvailable)+","+EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.QuoteCSV(row.unit)+","+status+","+verdict+","+
    String(row.windowStart)+","+String(row.windowStop)+","+String(row.everViolated)+","+witness+","+EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.QuoteCSV(row.message)
    else runId+" | "+row.metricId+" | "+status+" | "+verdict+
      (if row.valueAvailable then " | "+numeric+" "+row.unit else " | unavailable")+
      (if row.everViolated then " | first witness at "+witness+" s" else "")+
      (if row.message<>"" then " | "+row.message else "");
end FormatRow;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions;
function SafeIdentifier "Check an identifier used in an output filename"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input String value;
  output Boolean accepted;
protected
  String ch;
algorithm
  accepted:=Modelica.Utilities.Strings.length(value)>0;
  for i in 1:Modelica.Utilities.Strings.length(value) loop
    ch:=Modelica.Utilities.Strings.substring(value,i,i);
    accepted:=accepted and Modelica.Utilities.Strings.find("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_-",ch)>0;
  end for;
end SafeIdentifier;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions;
function SampleCount "Count a finite half-open window on an explicit sample grid"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input Modelica.Units.SI.Time left;
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time right;
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time origin;
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time period;
  output Integer n;
algorithm
  assert(period>0,"Sample period must be positive.");
  n:=max(0,integer(ceil((right-origin)/period-1e-9))-max(0,integer(ceil((left-origin)/period-1e-9))));
end SampleCount;

function ToVerdict "Decide requirement evidence without discarding a recorded witness"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus status;
  input Boolean everViolated;
  input Boolean isRequirement=true;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
algorithm

Project finite uniformly spaced samples onto a complex sinusoid

输入、输出和物理端口（9 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (9)
input Real x[:];
input Modelica.Units.SI.Time period;
input Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency f;
input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper taper=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper.Rectangular;
input Boolean removeMean=false;
output Real re;
output Real im;
output Real amplitude;
output Real phase(unit="rad");

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. Check its interfaces, translation diagnostics and results in a minimal model. An example in the same group is not complete validation of this class.

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions;
function Tone "Project finite uniformly spaced samples onto a complex sinusoid"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input Real x[:];
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time period;
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency f;
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper taper=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper.Rectangular;
  input Boolean removeMean=false;
  output Real re;
  output Real im;
  output Real amplitude;
  output Real phase(unit="rad");
protected
  Integer n=size(x,1);
  Real dc;
  Real w;
  Real wTotal;
  Real angle;
  Real scale;
algorithm
  assert(n>=16 and n<=1024 and period>0 and f>=0 and f<=1/(2*period),"Invalid finite spectral projection.");
  dc:=if removeMean then sum(x)/n else 0;
  re:=0; im:=0; wTotal:=0;
  for k in 1:n loop
    w:=if taper==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Taper.Rectangular then 1 else 0.5-0.5*cos(2*Modelica.Constants.pi*(k-1)/(n-1));
    angle:=2*Modelica.Constants.pi*f*(k-1)*period;
    re:=re+w*(x[k]-dc)*cos(angle);
    im:=im-w*(x[k]-dc)*sin(angle);
    wTotal:=wTotal+w;
  end for;
  scale:=(if f<=0 or f>=1/(2*period) then 1 else 2)/wTotal;
  re:=scale*re; im:=scale*im;
  amplitude:=sqrt(re^2+im^2);
  phase:=Modelica.Math.atan2(im,re);
end Tone;

function VerdictFromCode "Decode the fixed verdict ordinal"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
input Integer code;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict verdict;
algorithm
  verdict:=if code==1 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.UNDECIDED elseif code==2 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.SATISFIED elseif code==3 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.VIOLATED
    elseif code==5 then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.NOT_APPLICABLE else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.Verdict.INVALID;
end VerdictFromCode;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block ContinuousEvidence "连续窗口时长与失效证据"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  input Boolean dataOK "Already checked input validity and numerical domain";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput observedDuration(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) "Accumulated valid duration" ;
protected

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time simulationStart(fixed=false);
  Boolean invalidSeen;
  Boolean sufficient;

initial equation
  simulationStart = time;
  assert(windowEnd > windowStart, "Window must have positive duration.");

equation
  when {initial(),inWindow and not dataOK} then
    invalidSeen = inWindow and not dataOK;
  end when;
  der(observedDuration) = if inWindow and dataOK then 1 else 0;
  sufficient = observedDuration > 0;

  inWindow = applicable and time >= windowStart and time < windowEnd;
  dataState = if not applicable then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif invalidSeen then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif time < windowEnd then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING
    elseif simulationStart > windowStart or not sufficient then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE
    else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE;
end ContinuousEvidence;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
impure function LogCompletion "Print one audit message when an output has been finalised"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
  input String operation "Created, overwritten, skipped, or finalised artefact";
  input String outputType "CSV, text, console, in-memory table, or aggregate";
  input String destination "Absolute file path or non-file output location";
  input String runId="" "User-defined experiment identifier";
  input String resultSetId="" "Identifier of this result set";
  input Integer recordCount(min=0)=0 "Number of reported records";
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time simulationTime=0 "Simulation time when finalised";
  input String status="SUCCESS" "SUCCESS or SKIPPED";
  output Boolean logged;
protected
  Integer ms;
  Integer sec;
  Integer minute;
  Integer hour;
  Integer day;
  Integer month;
  Integer year;
  String timestamp;
algorithm
  (ms,sec,minute,hour,day,month,year):=Modelica.Utilities.System.getTime();
  timestamp:=String(year)+"-"+(if month<10 then "0" else "")+String(month)
    +"-"+(if day<10 then "0" else "")+String(day)
    +" "+(if hour<10 then "0" else "")+String(hour)
    +":"+(if minute<10 then "0" else "")+String(minute)
    +":"+(if sec<10 then "0" else "")+String(sec)
    +"."+(if ms<100 then "0" else "")+(if ms<10 then "0" else "")+String(ms);
  Modelica.Utilities.Streams.print(
    "[EvaluationLibrary]["+status+"] "+operation+"\n"
    +"  generated_at: "+timestamp+" (host local time)\n"
    +"  simulation_time: "+String(simulationTime,significantDigits=8)+" s\n"
    +"  output_type: "+outputType+"\n"
    +"  destination: "+destination+"\n"
    +"  run_id: "+runId+"\n"
    +"  result_set_id: "+resultSetId+"\n"
    +"  record_count: "+String(recordCount));
  logged:=true;
end LogCompletion;

Accumulate qualified samples with stable online variance

输入、输出和物理端口（10 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (10)
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u;
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput take;
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput count(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput average(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput m2(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput sumSquare(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput minimum(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput maximum(start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput argminTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true);
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput argmaxTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true);

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. Check its interfaces, translation diagnostics and results in a minimal model. An example in the same group is not complete validation of this class.

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block SampleAccumulator "Accumulate qualified samples with stable online variance"
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput take ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput count(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput average(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput m2(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput sumSquare(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput minimum(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput maximum(start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput argminTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput argmaxTime(unit="s",start=0,fixed=true) ;
algorithm
  when take then
    count:=pre(count)+1;
    average:=pre(average)+(u-pre(average))/count;
    m2:=pre(m2)+(u-pre(average))*(u-average);
    sumSquare:=pre(sumSquare)+u*u;
    minimum:=if pre(count)==0 or u<pre(minimum) then u else pre(minimum);
    maximum:=if pre(count)==0 or u>pre(maximum) then u else pre(maximum);
    argminTime:=if pre(count)==0 or u<pre(minimum) then time else pre(argminTime);
    argmaxTime:=if pre(count)==0 or u>pre(maximum) then time else pre(argmaxTime);
  end when;
end SampleAccumulator;

参数与默认声明（6 项）Parameters and declared defaults (6)

parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";
输入、输出和物理端口（5 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (5)
input Boolean dataOK "Already checked input validity and numerical domain";
Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window";
output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. Check its interfaces, translation diagnostics and results in a minimal model. An example in the same group is not complete validation of this class.

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block SampledEvidence "均匀采样半开窗及完整性证据"
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart = 0 "Inclusive window start, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowEnd = 5 "Exclusive window end, s";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether the analysis applies";

  input Boolean dataOK "Already checked input validity and numerical domain";

  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput inWindow "Current point lies in applicable window" ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState "Collection and data validity state";

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.02 "Sampling interval, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleOffset(min=0) = 0 "First tick offset from windowStart, s";
  parameter Integer minimumSamples(min=1) = 2 "Minimum valid observations";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput validSamples(start=0,fixed=true) "Accepted observations" ;

  output Boolean takeTick;
protected

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time simulationStart(fixed=false);
  Boolean invalidSeen;
  Boolean sufficient;

  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time firstSample = windowStart+sampleOffset;
  final parameter Real tickSpan = (windowEnd-firstSample)/samplePeriod;
  final parameter Integer expected = if tickSpan <= 0 then 0 else
    max(1,integer(ceil(tickSpan-8*Modelica.Constants.eps*max(1,abs(tickSpan)))));
  discrete Boolean badSample(start=false,fixed=true);

initial equation
  simulationStart = time;
  assert(windowEnd > windowStart, "Window must have positive duration.");

  assert(samplePeriod > 0 and sampleOffset >= 0 and sampleOffset < samplePeriod and minimumSamples >= 1,
    "Invalid sampling grid.");
equation
  takeTick = applicable and expected > 0 and noEvent(time < firstSample+(expected-0.5)*samplePeriod);
  when sample(firstSample,samplePeriod) then
    badSample = pre(badSample) or (takeTick and not dataOK);
    validSamples = pre(validSamples)+(if takeTick and dataOK then 1 else 0);
  end when;
  invalidSeen = badSample;
  sufficient = validSamples >= minimumSamples and validSamples == expected;

  inWindow = applicable and time >= windowStart and time < windowEnd;
  dataState = if not applicable then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE
    elseif invalidSeen then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif time < windowEnd then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING
    elseif simulationStart > windowStart or not sufficient then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE
    else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE;
end SampledEvidence;

Retain a declared finite set of same-grid samples

within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block SpectrumBuffer "Retain a declared finite set of same-grid samples"
parameter Integer n(min=16,max=1024)=200;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput take ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput ready ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanOutput overflow(start=false,fixed=true) ;
  output Real samples[n](each start=0,each fixed=true);
  output Integer count(start=0,fixed=true);
initial equation
  assert(n>=16 and n<=1024,"Spectral cache must contain 16 to 1024 samples.");
equation
  ready=count==n;
algorithm
  when take then
    if pre(count)<n then
      count:=pre(count)+1;
      samples[count]:=u;
    else overflow:=true;
    end if;
  end when;
end SpectrumBuffer;

within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block ToneSpectrum "有限均匀窗的一侧离散幅度谱和相位谱"
  parameter Integer nSamples(min=4,max=1024) = 128 "Uniform sample count";
  parameter Integer nBins(min=1,max=div(nSamples,2)) = min(32,div(nSamples,2)) "Highest retained nonnegative bin";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time startTime = 0 "First sample, s";
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=Modelica.Constants.small) = 0.01 "Tick period, s";
  parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow window = EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow.Rectangular "Weighting";
  parameter Real amplitudeFloor(min=0) = 1e-9 "Smallest reportable amplitude, input unit";
  parameter Boolean applicable = true "Whether this record applies";
  final parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency resolution = 1/(nSamples*samplePeriod) "Bin width, Hz";
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealInput u "Trajectory" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput valid "Clocked data validity" ;
  output Real amplitude[nBins+1] "One-sided peak amplitude, including DC";
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Angle phase[nBins+1] "Phase of cosine reference at startTime";
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Frequency frequency[nBins+1] = {(i-1)*resolution for i in 1:nBins+1};
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.IntegerOutput dominantBin "Strongest non-DC bin, or zero" ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.RealOutput dominantFrequency(unit="Hz") ;
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus dataState;
protected
  EvaluationLibrary.Response.SampleHistory history(n=nSamples,startTime=startTime,
    samplePeriod=samplePeriod,applicable=applicable);
  Real bins[nBins+1,2] "Discrete spectrum of the completed history";
  Real strongest;
initial equation
  assert(nSamples>=4 and nSamples<=1024 and nBins>=1 and nBins<=div(nSamples,2) and samplePeriod>0 and amplitudeFloor>=0,
    "Invalid DFT grid.");
equation
  history.u = u;
  history.valid = valid;
  dataState = history.dataState;
  bins = if dataState==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.oneSidedDFT(history.samples,nBins,window,amplitudeFloor) else zeros(nBins+1,2);
  amplitude = bins[:,1];
  phase = bins[:,2];
  dominantFrequency = dominantBin*resolution;
algorithm
  strongest := amplitudeFloor;
  dominantBin := 0;
  for k in 1:nBins loop
    if amplitude[k+1] > strongest then
      strongest := amplitude[k+1];
      dominantBin := k;
    end if;
  end for;
end ToneSpectrum;

实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
block WindowEvidence "Qualify the first requested window and track data coverage independently"
parameter EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode windowMode=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStart=0;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time windowStop=5;
  parameter Boolean applicable=true;
  parameter Boolean useValidInput=false;

  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time samplePeriod(min=0)=0.01;
  parameter Modelica.Units.SI.Time sampleStart=0;

  parameter Boolean sampled=false;
    Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput rawWindow ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput dataOK ;
  Modelica.Blocks.Interfaces.BooleanInput overflow ;
  output Boolean inWindow;
  output Boolean complete;
  output Boolean unexpectedWindow;
  output Boolean tick;
  output Boolean sampleInWindow;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time actualStart;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time actualStop;
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time observedDuration(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Modelica.Units.SI.Time validDuration(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Integer expectedSamples;
  output Integer receivedSamples(start=0,fixed=true);
  output Integer validSamples(start=0,fixed=true);
  output EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus status;
protected
  discrete Boolean entered(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean closed(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean secondWindow(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean terminalSeen(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Boolean invalidSeen(start=false,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time startObserved(start=0,fixed=true);
  discrete Modelica.Units.SI.Time stopObserved(start=0,fixed=true);
  Boolean windowClosed;
  Boolean invalidNow;
  Boolean fullCoverage;
  Integer gridIndex;
  Integer firstIndex;
  Integer stopIndex;
initial equation
  assert(windowStop>=windowStart,"Reversed evaluation window.");
  assert(samplePeriod>0,"Sample period must be positive.");
equation
  when {initial(),change(rawWindow)} then
    entered=(if initial() then false else pre(entered)) or rawWindow;
    closed=not initial() and (pre(closed) or (pre(entered) and not rawWindow));
    secondWindow=not initial() and (pre(secondWindow) or (rawWindow and pre(closed)));
    startObserved=if rawWindow and (initial() or not pre(entered)) then time else pre(startObserved);
    stopObserved=if not rawWindow and not initial() and pre(entered) and not pre(closed) then time else pre(stopObserved);
  end when;
  when terminal() then terminalSeen=true; end when;
  actualStart=if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime then windowStart else startObserved;
  actualStop=if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime then windowStop else if closed then stopObserved else if entered then time else 0;
  windowClosed=if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime then time>=windowStop else closed;
  inWindow=applicable and (if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime then time>=windowStart and time<windowStop else entered and not closed and rawWindow);
  complete=not applicable or windowClosed or terminalSeen;
  unexpectedWindow=windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.ExternalSingle and secondWindow;
  invalidNow=inWindow and not dataOK;
  when {initial(),invalidNow,overflow,unexpectedWindow} then
    invalidSeen=(not initial() and pre(invalidSeen)) or invalidNow or overflow or unexpectedWindow;
  end when;
  der(observedDuration)=if inWindow then 1 else 0;
  der(validDuration)=if inWindow and dataOK then 1 else 0;
  tick=sampled and sample(sampleStart,samplePeriod);
  gridIndex=noEvent(integer(floor((time-sampleStart)/samplePeriod+0.5)));
  firstIndex=max(0,integer(ceil((windowStart-sampleStart)/samplePeriod-1e-9)));
  stopIndex=max(0,integer(ceil((windowStop-sampleStart)/samplePeriod-1e-9)));
  sampleInWindow=applicable and (if windowMode==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WindowMode.FixedTime then gridIndex>=firstIndex and gridIndex<stopIndex else inWindow);
  when tick then
    receivedSamples=pre(receivedSamples)+(if sampleInWindow then 1 else 0);
    validSamples=pre(validSamples)+(if sampleInWindow and dataOK then 1 else 0);
  end when;
  expectedSamples=if sampled then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.SampleCount(actualStart,actualStop,sampleStart,samplePeriod) else 0;
  fullCoverage=windowClosed and actualStop>actualStart and observedDuration>=actualStop-actualStart-1e-7
    and (not sampled or (expectedSamples>0 and validSamples==expectedSamples));
  status=if not applicable then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE elseif invalidSeen then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INVALID
    elseif not complete then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.PENDING elseif fullCoverage then EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.COMPLETE else EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.DataStatus.INCOMPLETE;
end WindowEvidence;

within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
impure function WriteRows "Write one complete result set and report its outcome to the terminal"
  extends Modelica.Icons.Function;
  input String path "Output path; ignored for console output";
  input String runId;
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.MetricResult records[:];
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WritePolicy writePolicy=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WritePolicy.RejectExisting;
  input Boolean csv=false;
  input Boolean console=false;
  input String resultSetId="" "Identifier included in the audit message";
  input Modelica.Units.SI.Time simulationTime=0 "Simulation instant of export";
  input Boolean logToConsole=true "Log success or skip once per call";
  output Boolean written;
  output Boolean skippedExisting;
protected
  Boolean exists;
  Boolean logged;
  String target;
  String formatName;
  String action;
algorithm
  assert(console or Modelica.Utilities.Strings.length(path)>0,
    "WriteRows requires a non-empty destination path for file exports.");
  target:=if console then "" else path;
  formatName:=if csv then "CSV" elseif console then "console text" else "text";
  exists:=if console then false else Modelica.Utilities.Files.exist(path);
  skippedExisting:=exists and writePolicy==EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.WritePolicy.RejectExisting;
  written:=false;

  if not skippedExisting then
    if exists then
      Modelica.Utilities.Files.removeFile(path);
    end if;
    if csv then
      Modelica.Utilities.Streams.print(
        "runId,metricId,requirementVersion,value,valueAvailable,unit,dataStatus,verdict,windowStart,windowStop,everViolated,firstViolationTime,message",target);
    end if;
    for i in 1:size(records,1) loop
      Modelica.Utilities.Streams.print(
        EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Functions.FormatRow(runId,records[i],csv),target);
    end for;
    if not console then
      Modelica.Utilities.Streams.close(path);
      assert(Modelica.Utilities.Files.exist(path),
        "WriteRows could not confirm the requested output file: "+path);
    end if;
    written:=true;
  end if;

  if logToConsole then
    action:=if skippedExisting then "Export skipped: destination already exists"
      elseif console then "Console result set generated"
      elseif exists then "Result file overwritten" else "Result file created";
    logged:=EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.LogCompletion(
      action,formatName,
      if console then "terminal (stdout)" else Modelica.Utilities.Files.fullPathName(path),
      runId,resultSetId,size(records,1),simulationTime,
      if skippedExisting then "SKIPPED" else "SUCCESS");
  end if;
end WriteRows;

原始源码描述（保留原语言）Original source description (original language retained)

生成文件或可读摘要所需的字符串

输入、输出和物理端口（5 项）Inputs, outputs and physical connectors (5)

没有顶层示例直接引用此类；它可能通过内部辅助模型被使用。请先在最小独立模型中检查接口、翻译诊断与结果，勿把同组示例当成此类的完整验证。No top-level example directly references this class; it may be used through a helper. 实现与使用契约：阅读本元件源码Implementation and usage contract: inspect the source
within EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal;
function formatResults "生成文件或可读摘要所需的字符串"
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.LegacyMetricResult results[:];
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.ReportFormat format;
  input String title;
  input Boolean includeHeader = true;
  output String content;
protected
  String row;
  String numeric;
  String stamp;
algorithm
  if format == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.ReportFormat.CSV then
    content := if includeHeader then "metric,unit,value,defined,window_start_s,window_end_s,observed_at_s,data_state,status,explanation" else "";
    for i in 1:size(results,1) loop
      numeric := if results[i].defined then String(results[i].value,significantDigits=12) else "";
      stamp := if results[i].defined then String(results[i].observedAt,significantDigits=12) else "";
      row := EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.csvQuote(results[i].metric) + "," + EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.csvQuote(results[i].unit) +
        "," + numeric + "," + String(results[i].defined) +
        "," + String(results[i].windowStart,significantDigits=12) + "," + String(results[i].windowEnd,significantDigits=12) +
        "," + stamp + "," + String(results[i].dataState) + "," + String(results[i].status) +
        "," + EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Internal.csvQuote(results[i].explanation);
      content := if Modelica.Utilities.Strings.isEmpty(content) then row else content + "\n" + row;
    end for;
  else
    content := title;
    for i in 1:size(results,1) loop
      numeric := if results[i].defined then String(results[i].value,significantDigits=8) + " " + results[i].unit else "unavailable";
      content := content + "\n" + results[i].metric + " = " + numeric +
        " [" + String(results[i].status) + ", " + String(results[i].dataState) + "]" +
        " from " + String(results[i].windowStart) + " to " + String(results[i].windowEnd) + " s. " +
        results[i].explanation;
    end for;
  end if;
end formatResults;

function oneSidedDFT "一侧 DFT、相干增益校正和主峰"
  input Real x[:];
  input Integer nBins "Highest nonnegative bin";
  input EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow window;
  input Real amplitudeFloor;
  output Real bins[nBins+1,2] "Amplitude and phase";
protected
  Integer n = size(x,1);
  Real w[size(x,1)];
  Real coherentSum;
  Real a;
  Real b;
  Real angle;
algorithm
  for j in 1:n loop
    w[j] := if window == EvaluationLibrary.Utilities.Types.SpectralWindow.Hann then
      0.5*(1-cos(2*Modelica.Constants.pi*(j-1)/n)) else 1;
  end for;
  coherentSum := sum(w);
  for k in 0:nBins loop
    a := 0;
    b := 0;
    for j in 1:n loop
      angle := 2*Modelica.Constants.pi*k*(j-1)/n;
      a := a+w[j]*x[j]*cos(angle);
      b := b-w[j]*x[j]*sin(angle);
    end for;
    bins[k+1,1] := (if k==0 or 2*k==n then 1 else 2)*sqrt(a^2+b^2)/coherentSum;
    bins[k+1,2] := if bins[k+1,1]>amplitudeFloor then Modelica.Math.atan2(b,a) else 0;
  end for;
end oneSidedDFT;
